# Consistent Problems

Systemic defects that affect **many forms in the same way** — a wrong fragment
used everywhere, an old-engine bug since fixed, a layout convention that changed.
Distinct from [resolved.md](resolved.md), which records one-off, per-feedback-item
resolutions.

Each entry is **data** the `/sweep` engine executes — symptom, how to find
affected forms, how to fix one. The engine is problem-agnostic: it dispatches on
the `Detect` / `Fix` verbs below and has no per-problem logic. A new entry is
**code-free only when it can reuse an existing `Detect`/`Fix` script**; a new
shape (a structural rewrite, an attribute target no regex matches) needs a new,
committed, reviewed `find_`/`set_` script first. See `.claude/skills/sweep/SKILL.md`
→ "Record a new problem".

**Establishing "correct":** a sweep needs a ground-truth reference. The conversion
engine is **not** authoritative by default (it has known faults) — rely on it only
when the user says it's right for this problem; otherwise ask for a reference.

## Entry format

```markdown
## PROBLEM-<slug> — <title>
**Symptom:** what's wrong, and where it shows up in the form .content.xml
**Detect:** grep: <regex>            # how to identify an affected form's deployed XML
            # or → script: <name> <args>   (when a regex can't express it)
**Fix:**    manual: <AI procedure>   # how to fix ONE form
            # or → script: <name> <args>   (deterministic, worth scripting)
**Verify:** must: <regex> ; must-not: <regex>   # optional auto-check (check_form.py) after the fix
**Max changed lines:** <N>                       # optional blast-radius guard (form_diff.py): flag forms whose fix touched more than N lines. Attribute-level fixes only — for additive/structural fixes set it to the expected per-instance insert size, or omit and rely on Verify / the fix's `ok`.
**Runs automatically:** yes | no                 # the switch — the only field any script reads
**Corpus fixed:** yes | no — <what is still missing>   # the human truth about the 304 packages
**Origin:** feedback-recurrence | engine-diff | manual
**Tracking:** #<n>                   # the consistent-problem GitHub issue
**Status:** open | piloted <YYYY-MM-DD> (N/TOTAL) | swept <YYYY-MM-DD>
```

**`Runs automatically:`** — the switch, and **the only field any script reads to decide it**. `yes`
means `apply_sweeps` applies this entry's Fix, CI's `check_regressions` checks it and the intake app
offers it. `no` means the tooling ignores the entry completely — the code may exist and be perfectly
good, it is simply not wired in. This replaces the old test for the word "swept" inside `Status:`,
which meant "wired in" and "finished" at once and so said neither clearly.

**`Corpus fixed:`** — the truth about the 304 packages, for humans, read by nothing. `yes` = every
form is fixed and deployed. `no` = say how many still violate it and what is missing (a pilot, a
visual review, a re-sweep). Absent = nobody has stated it; read `Status:` and assume nothing.
**Never write `yes` because the code works — write it when the forms are actually fixed.**

**Verbs**
- `Detect: grep: <regex>` — the default; matched against each form's
  `.content.xml` by `find_affected.py`.
- `Fix: manual: <procedure>` — the default; the sweep agent edits each form's XML
  with judgment (extract → edit → repack), same discipline as the feedback Worker.
- `Fix: script: <name> <args>` — a deterministic fix. The script takes the form's
  ZIP, transforms its `.content.xml` in place, and prints a JSON report including a
  uniform **`ok`** boolean (form is now in the target state) that `run_sweep`'s
  verify consumes. Existing fixers to reuse: `swap_fragment` (fragRef value),
  `remove_attr` (drop an attribute), `set_attr_on` (ensure an attribute on
  selector-matched elements, quote-aware), `set_fragment_panel` (fragRef + panel
  attributes), `set_dor_template` (entity-aware DoR config). A new *shape* (e.g. a
  structural rewrite that synthesises nested nodes) needs a new such script.
- The whole staged run is executed by `run_sweep.py --slug PROBLEM-<x>
  [--pilot|--continue|--only]`, which reads this entry and does detect → fix →
  delta → diff → verify → deploy → issue row → one PR.

**Two traps in how this file is parsed — both fail silently (learned the hard way 2026-07-27, parsers unified 2026-08-26):**
- **Detect and Fix args are both `shlex.split()` — quote like a shell.** `run_sweep.py`, `apply_sweeps.py`
  and `check_regressions.py::detector_argv()` all split the verb inside the backticks with `shlex.split()` and
  there is no shell, so a value that contains a double quote must be wrapped in single quotes:
  `--select 'name="PN_BR"'`. A bare `name="PN_BR"` is stripped to `name=PN_BR` and matches nothing —
  **the rule reports 0 affected and looks green**. (Until 2026-08-26 `check_regressions` used plain `.split()`,
  so the two verbs needed *opposite* quoting and a quoted selector could not be written for both; the margin
  and checkbox entries carry the scars. Cost of the original trap: 258 affected forms reported clean, #55
  unenforced for weeks.) Quote-free tokens (`--select controls/metadata`) split the same either way.
- **The on/off switch is the `Runs automatically:` field** — see "Entry format" above. When an entry has
  no such field the parsers fall back to the old test, the substring `"swept"` in the `Status:` line, and
  print which entries were read that way, so a rule merged from a branch that predates the field behaves
  exactly as before. Consequence: in an entry with **no** `Runs automatically:` line, any wording that
  merely *mentions* "swept" in `Status:` still enrols the rule in the guard and `apply_sweeps` — and
  `not swept` mentions it too. Give every new entry the field explicitly and the trap disappears.

## PROBLEM-banking-relationship-fragment — Banking-relationship panel: correct fragment + DoR exclusion
**Symptom:** the banking-relationship panel (the one bearing the BankingRelationship `fragRef`) needs two things the conversion engine emits: (1) the **one correct fragment** — `affrg_BankingRelationship1` (wrong forms use germany/italy/global variants or a dam-path reference; the correct one shows "UBS Europe SE" under Bankbeziehung), and (2) **`dorExclusion="true"`** so the whole panel is excluded from the Document of Record. The fix sets both on that panel; everything else on it (resourceType, name, dorExcludeTitle/Description) is left as-is.
**Detect:** script: `find_panel_noncanonical.py --match-frag BankingRelationship --exclude-frag CustodyAccount --require-frag /content/forms/af/afforms_ubs_fragmentlib/affrg_BankingRelationship1 --require-attr dorExclusion=true`  _(a form is affected if its banking panel lacks the canonical fragRef or `dorExclusion="true"`)_
**Fix:** script: `set_fragment_panel.py --match-frag BankingRelationship --exclude-frag CustodyAccount --set-frag /content/forms/af/afforms_ubs_fragmentlib/affrg_BankingRelationship1 --set-attr dorExclusion=true`
**Verify:** must: `fragRef="/content/forms/af/afforms_ubs_fragmentlib/affrg_BankingRelationship1"` ; must-not: `affrg_(germany|italiy|global)_BankingRelationship`  _(the per-panel `dorExclusion` is asserted by the fix's `all_canonical:true`, since a file-level grep can't tell which panel carries it)_
**Max changed lines:** 3   _(fragRef swap = 1 changed line, `dorExclusion` insert = +1 → up to 3 diff lines for a panel missing both; flag anything more, incl. the one multi-panel form)_
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** feedback-recurrence
**Tracking:** #10
**Status:** swept 2026-06-24 (212/212 fixed + deployed; 5 flagged diff>3, all verified benign — 4 had `dorExclusion="false"`→`true` plus the fragRef swap, AANB_019 has 3 banking panels). Open follow-up: whether the legacy custom UBS panel honors `dorExclusion` in the rendered DoR (the ~99 custom-panel forms may need the panel switched to the standard type for the exclusion to visually take effect; authored value is already correct).

**Scope note — summary exclusion deliberately NOT swept (2026-06-23):** the engine
*also* emits `summaryExclusion="true"` on this panel, but its summary-exclusion
output isn't correct yet, so we leave the deployed `summaryExclusion` exactly as-is
until the engine is fixed. This sweep enforces only the fragment + `dorExclusion`.

**Follow-up (2026-07-27):** the panel *containing* the banking fragment must also carry `css="ubs-margin-20"`. That panel is #29's `PN_BR` wrapper, so it lives in its own entry — [PROBLEM-banking-relationship-margin](#) — since this one is already done and `run_sweep` dispatches one Detect/Fix pair per slug. Nothing here changes.

**Review note:** "UBS Europe SE" appears legitimately all over these forms
(signatures, legal text, document titles) — do **not** auto-strip it. After the
swap, the per-form render-verify must inspect the deployed form for (a) a
**duplicate** "UBS Europe SE" under the banking section, and (b) **leftover inline
clearing/bank fields** (forms that had a custom-control banking panel). Flag ⚠️
for manual touch-up if either appears. `*_BankingRelationship_CustodyAccount` is a
different fragment — leave it alone (the detector/fix already exclude it).

## PROBLEM-dor-exclude-hidden — Uncheck "Exclude hidden fields from Document of Record"
**Symptom:** the DoR config (`dorProperties`) has `excludeFromDoRIfHidden="true"`, so hidden fields/sections are dropped from the Document of Record. It should be unchecked — the conversion engine omits the attribute entirely, and the 181 unchecked forms simply lack it.
**Detect:** grep: `excludeFromDoRIfHidden="true"`
**Fix:** script: `remove_attr.py --attr excludeFromDoRIfHidden`
**Verify:** must-not: `excludeFromDoRIfHidden=`
**Max changed lines:** 1   _(removing the attribute deletes exactly one line; flag any form whose diff touches more)_
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** feedback-recurrence
**Tracking:** #8
**Status:** swept 2026-06-23 (105/105, all checks passed)

## PROBLEM-jump-to-field-button — Jump-to-field button on the step-title panel + canonical title-node config
**Symptom:** the "Show jump to field button" option must be set on the **step-title panel** (`PN_FooTitle`) — in its **Summary** section — **not** on the title-draw. The engine wrongly emits `jumpToFieldButtonVisible="true"` on the `css="stepTitle"` title-draw (`panel.xml:55`), where it has no effect (**engine bug** — should move to the `panel_title` node). The old #12 sweep targeted the draw and was closed. The Summary section only exists on the UBS custom panel, so **[PROBLEM-panel-type-ubs](#) / #20 is a prerequisite** (merged 2026-07-01). Beyond jump-to-field this normalizes the title node to the shape the owner validated in AEM. FormConfigurator title panels keep their intentional DoR/summary exclusions (we only *add* on the panel, never strip).

Per step-title panel + its `headingLevel="2"` draw — **two cases**:

**Normal steps:**
- **panel** `PN_FooTitle`: add `jumpToFieldButtonVisible="true"` (791) + `dorExcludeDescription="true"`; **strip "rest to false"** — remove `summaryExclusion`/`dorExcludeTitle`/`dorExclusion` if present (7 panels).
- **draw** `TTL_Foo`: **remove** `jumpToFieldButtonVisible` (286) + add `summaryExclusion="true"` (210) + `dorExclusion="true"` (57).

**Steps with nothing to fill in get no button either (owner, 2026-08-10).** The button jumps back to a field, so a step that is pure text — legal provisions, a declaration, terms — has nothing to jump to. A step counts as text-only when no `guideTextBox`/`guideCheckBox`/`guideDatePicker`/`guideRadioButton`/`guideDropDownList`/`guideNumericBox`/`guideTelephone`/`guideScribble` appears anywhere inside it **and** it holds no `fragRef`. A fragment counts as input: its fields live in the fragment's own package, not in this repo, and in the corpus these are signature, banking and address fragments, all fillable. Such a step is otherwise treated as a normal step — same title-node config, and it still shows in the summary; only `jumpToFieldButtonVisible` is left off, and stripped if an earlier run of this sweep added it. **177 steps in 102 forms** carried one wrongly.

**Form configurator** (the "Formular Adressat" config step — **not** real content; **only ever the first page**, so matched only on `step_idx == 0` to avoid false hits on later steps — detected by `formconfig`/`configurator` in the step / title-panel / **title-draw** name, or `css="ubs-margin-10"` on the step; **124 across the corpus**):
- **NO** `jumpToFieldButtonVisible` (config step gets no jump button — it would otherwise also show in the summary jump-list).
- **title panel** `PN_FooTitle` (the panel around the configurator title): add `dorExclusion="true"` (73) + `summaryExclusion="true"` (111) + `dorExcludeDescription="true"` → excluded from both the summary and the DoR. Never strip. (On the **panel**, like jump-to-field — not the step panel, not the draw.)
- **draw**: same as normal (strip jtf, add summaryExclusion + dorExclusion).
- First-page gate matters: without it, a later `PN_FormSection` (AAOF/AAOM/AAPR step 1) or a signature step with `css="ubs-margin-10"` (BAZA/BAZC step 4) would be misread as a configurator.

**This rule OWNS `jumpToFieldButtonVisible`, and it is the only one that writes it (Marco's AAOV_033 report, 2026-08-21).** PROBLEM-repeating-panel carried the attribute in its own `TARGET_ATTRS` and stamped it on every repeatable it normalised. It runs **before** this rule in the file but writes a different node — a repeating panel, not a step-title panel — so nothing here ever took it back off, and the corpus drifted: pristine, **1'389** panels carry the button and only **27** are repeatables and **4** are generic signature panels; after the sweeps that becomes **612** and **128**. Symptom Marco saw: an edit button at the top of every signature block on AAOV_033's summary page. The attribute left that rule's `TARGET_ATTRS`, and a repeatable carrying a `fragRef` had it **stripped** as well. **That second half is superseded by the 2026-08-24 directive below:** a repeatable carrying the button is now correct, `DROP_ATTRS_FRAGMENT` is empty, and this rule writes the attribute on repeatables as well as on step-title panels. Not a marker check: the strip is re-derived from the tree on every pass, so a button re-added by hand or by an older script is taken off again.

**The configurator is not always the step's FIRST title panel (same report).** `title_panel(step)` returns the panel around the step's first `headingLevel="2"` draw, and `is_formconfig` was asked about that one only. AAOV_033's first step holds **three** title panels with the configurator **second**, so it was never examined — `fc_count: 0`, and its title panel never got the three exclusions. **125 configurator panels in the corpus, 61 of them unexcluded** for this reason. Both sides now walk `title_panels(step)` — every h2 draw of the first step that has a panel of its own — and treat whichever one answers `is_formconfig` as the configurator; the primary title panel keeps the normal step treatment either way.

**A text-only step gets the button off its STEP panel too (same report).** The strip above only ever looked at the *title* panel, but **30** of the corpus's 194 text-only steps carry the attribute on the **step panel** itself, which renders the same button. Both nodes are now stripped on a step with nothing to fill in (AAOV_033: `PN_Dichiarazione_2c49eddc`, `PN_Info_Last`).

**The STEP panel must never carry the button when the step has a title panel (Marco's AAOV_033 summary, 2026-08-24).** The strip above was written for text-only steps only — it sits inside that branch — so on a **normal** step nothing ever looked at the step panel: the engine's `jumpToFieldButtonVisible` stayed on `PN_Foo` while this rule added a second one to `PN_FooTitle` inside it, and AEM renders both — **two identical Edit links above every step heading in the summary**. The button never belongs to the step panel itself — it goes on the step-title panel, or on the step's repeatables once the 2026-08-24 directive below applies — so it comes off the step panel on **every** step that has a title panel, whatever kind of step it is. A step with **no** dedicated title panel is still skipped and keeps its button — that is the only one it has — unless it is a configurator or a text-only step, which must offer none at all (4 steps; the branch also now covers a step with no `headingLevel="2"` draw at all, which the fixer used to `continue` past — `BAAD_019:PN_Formconfig_b8a62724`). **299 step panels in 93 forms** carry one: 232 duplicating a title-panel button, 37 configurator, 30 text-only. The title-panel and draw sides are already clean (`panel_add_jtf`, `panel_strip_jtf`, `draw_strip_jtf` all 0). **Not caused by the repeating-panel or fragment-consolidation rules** — neither writes or keeps the attribute — and **no sweep reordering is needed**: `apply_sweeps` runs the registry to a fixpoint, so this rule re-runs after them even though it sits earlier in the file (20-form sample: ≤ 4 passes, second invocation byte-identical). `set_step_title_panel.py` also stopped emitting `jumpToFieldButtonVisible="true"` on the title draw it synthesises — this rule owns the attribute and strips it from every draw anyway.

**A DISABLED input is not something to fill in (owner directive 2026-09-01).** "Nothing to fill in" was
read as "no input of the right `guideNodeClass`", so a page holding only text plus text boxes or
dropdowns carrying `enabled="{Boolean}false"` was treated as a normal step and given a button. In
practice nothing on such a page can be modified, so there is nothing to jump back to. `is_text_only`
now ignores an input whose static `enabled` attribute says false (both the `{Boolean}false` and the
bare `false` spelling). **Only the STATIC attribute counts** — a field some rule disables at run time
still reads as fillable, because a rule can flip it back and no scan can settle which way it ends up.
A fragment panel's own `enabled` is not read either: what it does to the fields inside it cannot be
seen from this repo. Corpus measurement: **exactly 3 steps flip** — AAOE_033 `PN_Agreement` (the
declaration Marco reported: its only field is a disabled `Nazionalità` dropdown echoing a value
entered earlier), AAAM_019 `PN_OWnership` and AAAM_019 `PN_Section3` (16 disabled inputs) — no script
in either form re-enables those fields, `text_only` 186 -> 189, `panel_strip_jtf` 289 -> 292, and no
new form becomes affected (all three were already). `set_jump_to_field.py` had its own second copy of
`is_text_only`; it now imports the detector's, because a second copy is how the two sides drift and
the fixer would re-add a button the detector wants gone on every intake run.

137 title nodes already correct; 4 steps have no title panel (skip). **251 forms affected.**
**A second `h2` in a step is a section heading, not a title node (owner directive 2026-09-02).** The configurator test accepted the *step's* name and the engine's `css="ubs-margin-10"` for **every** title panel of the first step, so on a configurator step each section heading was treated as the configurator and given its DoR and summary exclusions — reported on AABF_019, where `Ordering Customer` (its own panel, `jcr:title` and `h2` draw, a heading the DoR must show) was excluded from the DoR and the summary and carried a stray jump-to-field button. A title panel other than the step's own is now the configurator only when **it** says so (`is_formconfig_node`: `formconfig`/`configurator` in the panel's or draw's own name — AAOV_033's second-position configurator still matches, 4 in the corpus); the other **144** are content, and this rule now says what a section heading looks like:
- the panel is never DoR- or summary-excluded, and loses those flags if an earlier pass wrote them (**73**);
- neither the panel nor its draw carries `jumpToFieldButtonVisible` (**18** + **6**) — the step's own title panel or the step's repeatables carry the button, which is the 2026-08-24 placement rule;
- the heading reaches the DoR **once**: where the panel renders its own `jcr:title` (104 of the 144, and `dorExcludeTitle` counts as not rendering) the draw keeps its exclusions or the DoR would show it twice; where the panel renders nothing the draw loses them (**31**).
A panel with occurrence bounds is skipped whatever else it holds — it is PROBLEM-repeating-panel's node and the button belongs on it; stripping it here and adding it there is a loop that never settles (AACP_019 `PN_CPGRP_ADR`, caught by the idempotence check).
**Detect:** script: `find_jump_to_field.py`  _(per step-title panel/draw; emits `fix[]` {form,step,panel,deltas} + counts; affected = forms with any delta. `text_only` counts the steps with nothing to fill in, `panel_strip_jtf` the buttons to remove from a title panel, and `step_strip_jtf` the ones to remove from the STEP panel)_
**Fix:** script: `set_jump_to_field.py`  _(byte-offset, quote-aware open-tag scan for rich-text `_value`; adds the panel attrs, strips jtf from the draw, adds draw attrs; prints `{inner, changed:[…], ok}`)_
**Verify:** the fix script's `ok` (well-formed) + a re-scan showing 0 deltas remain + a pilot **visual spot-check** (jump-to-field button now renders on the step; title shows once).
**Max changed lines:** omit — mixed add/strip across two nodes per step; gate on the fix's `ok` + re-scan.
**Where the button lives, restated (owner directive 2026-08-24).** The rule is now about the PAGE,
not only about its title:

- the page has **at least one repeatable panel** -> the button goes on **every repeatable**, and the
  step-title panel gives it up. A repeatable renders one per instance, which is what a person
  actually wants to jump back to;
- the page has **no repeatable** -> the step-title panel keeps it, exactly as before;
- the page is **only text** -> no button anywhere. Unchanged.

**Every** repeatable, nested ones included — asked explicitly, Marco chose that over outermost-only.
Corpus: 897 repeatables, 54 inside another; 1164 wizard steps, 194 text-only, 423 holding a
repeatable. Pristine detector delta: `rep_add_jtf` **625** across **311** steps, `panel_strip_jtf`
**308** title panels giving it up, `rep_strip_jtf` 17 (repeatables in a text-only step), affected
forms 103 -> 236. New counters `rep_add_jtf` / `rep_strip_jtf` / `steps_with_repeatable`; the fixer's
`changed[]` rows gain `rep_add` / `rep_strip`.

**The repeatables' button is NOT gated on the form configurator.** `is_formconfig` is a verdict about
a step's FIRST title panel, and on AARG_033 the whole first page sits inside
`PN_FormConfigurator_a2439641` — the configurator radio *and* the account-holder rows. Gating there
suppressed the button on `PN_CPGRP` and `PN_AHGRP`, the two panels a person would most want to reach.
The configurator's own title panel still gets no button and keeps its summary/DoR exclusions, which
is what "el form configurator queda excluido de la summary page" means.

**`repeating_panel.DROP_ATTRS_FRAGMENT` had to be emptied.** It deleted `jumpToFieldButtonVisible`
from any repeatable carrying a `fragRef` — reasoning from 2026-08-21 that "a fragment panel is never
a step". Under the new rule a repeatable carrying the button is correct, and after the consolidation
most repeatables ARE fragment panels, so the two rules would have taken turns adding and deleting it
for ever. That rule now neither writes nor removes the attribute anywhere; `phase_bounds` still drops
it from a panel it takes the BOUNDS off, which is right — that panel has stopped repeating and this
rule puts the button on whatever repeats instead. Verified: 20-form sample converges, second
invocation byte-identical, re-scan 0 affected.

**Corpus fixed: NO — a re-sweep is pending.** The 2026-08-24 placement directive moves the button to the
repeatables, and **236 forms violate the new rule** with no Skip line covering them — master's
full-corpus backstop would fail for every one. "swept" on the Status line means enrolled, not
done.
**Runs automatically:** no
**Origin:** engine-diff (engine misplaces it on the draw; correct target is the panel)
**Tracking:** #22 (supersedes closed #12)
**Status:** **section-heading rule added 2026-09-02 — 36 forms to re-sweep** (73 panel exclusions, 24 stray buttons, 31 draw exclusions; every fixed form re-scans clean, a second run is a no-op, and `blueprint:validate_aem_package_from_file` on AABF_019 is 11 -> 11, no new category). Previously: **swept 2026-07-02 (254/254 fixed + deployed, PR #26 merged)** — jump-to-field on the step-title panel; canonical title-node config; form-configurator (first-page) gets no button + title panel excluded from summary+DoR. Engine still misplaces jtf on the draw (upstream fix pending). **Re-sweep pending 2026-08-24**: the step-panel duplicate above — 299 steps / 93 forms, 103 forms affected in total; `check_regressions` is red for those until it runs.

## PROBLEM-dor-custom-template — DoR config: custom template (metaTemplateRef) + "Use Adaptive Form Title"
Two DoR-config fixes the engine emits, applied together (all **other** master-page properties are left exactly as-is, per request 2026-06-24):
1. **Custom template** — `metaTemplateRef` on `dorProperties` should be the entity-General template, not the generic Blank. Engine branches on entity (config.toml): 019 → `…/02_forms/UBS_General_Germany_DOR.xdp`, 033 → `UBS_General_Italy_DOR.xdp` (else, e.g. CH 001 → `UBS_Blank_DoR.xdp`, so Blank is correct there). 93 forms have Blank.
2. **Form Title = "Use Adaptive Form Title"** — the Header `AF_FORM_TITLE` node should be `valueFrom="formTitle"` with **no** `value` and **no** `fd:translationIds` (the engine's exact output). 31 forms deviate (29 hold a hardcoded `value` with `valueFrom="     "`, 2 use `valueFrom="template"`). The 44 forms with **no** `AF_FORM_TITLE` node (minimal master-page) are skipped — we don't synthesize the node ("leave properties as they are").

**Combined affected:** 121 forms (90 template-only, 28 form-title-only, 3 both; 165 already correct).
**Detect:** script: `find_dor_config.py` _(to build for --continue: affected if `metaTemplateRef` basename is `UBS_Blank_DoR.xdp` on a 019/033 form, OR `AF_FORM_TITLE` is present and not already `valueFrom="formTitle"` with no value/translationIds)_
**Fix:** script: `set_dor_template.py --replace-basename UBS_Blank_DoR.xdp --use-adaptive-form-title`  _(entity from JCR path; metaTemplateRef only changes Blank→General; `--use-adaptive-form-title` sets AF_FORM_TITLE valueFrom=formTitle and drops value+translationIds; no-ops anything already correct)_
**Verify:** must: `metaTemplateRef="[^"]*UBS_General_(Germany|Italy)_DOR\.xdp"` ; must-not: `UBS_Blank_DoR\.xdp` — **plus** the AF_FORM_TITLE node must be `valueFrom="formTitle"` with no value/translationIds (checked directly, since a file-level grep can't isolate one node). The fix reports `changed` + `form_title_changed`.
**Max changed lines:** 6   _(metaTemplate replace = 2; AF_FORM_TITLE: drop value + drop translationIds + valueFrom replace ≈ up to 4)_
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** engine-diff
**Tracking:** #14
**Status:** swept 2026-06-24 (121/121 — combined fix; AAXC_019 + BAAD_019 had only the Form Title set, their out-of-scope Custody/Blank-BankingRelationship templates correctly left untouched). Verify-check note: the `must=General` template assertion false-flags form-title-only forms whose template is a non-General variant — tighten to "template==General OR was not Blank" if this sweep is re-run.

**Scope note — only `UBS_Blank_DoR.xdp` (custom template) + the Form Title property are swept.** All other master-page properties (`ShowBankingRelationship`, `senderAddressTitle`, `APPCode`, structural nodes, `AF_HEADER_TEXT`, etc.) are **left untouched** per the 2026-06-24 decision. Other non-General templates (`UBS_Blank_Letter_DoR.xdp` 44, `UBS_Custody_<entity>` 8, `UBS_Blank_BankingRelationship` 2) are also left as-is.

**Rollout note — overlaps other sweeps.** ~90% of these 93 forms are also in the banking (#10) and jump-to-field (#12) affected sets — **0 are free of both**. Separate sweep branches that edit the same `_merged.zip` conflict at merge (binary/LFS, no 3-way merge). **Sequence the rollouts**: fully merge one sweep, rebase the others on the new master, then `--continue` (all three fixes are idempotent, so re-running on the merged base is safe). Pilots are chosen to avoid forms already committed to another sweep branch, so no conflict arises before rollout.

---

## PROBLEM-step-title-panel — Each wizard step's title wrapped in a step-title panel
**Symptom:** the engine wraps every wizard **step**'s title in a dedicated *step-title panel* (`guidePanel` named `{step}Title`, holding a `headingLevel="2"` title-draw) as the step's **first child** (engine `profiles/ubs/aem/panel.xml`; **engine confirmed authoritative for this problem** by the owner — *but only for the wrap structure; the engine does NOT reliably detect the title text, so title text comes from the deployed form, never the engine*). A *step* = a panel directly under `guideContainer › rootPanel(guideRootPanel) › items`, excluding the specials (`summary`/`preview`/`formmetadata`/`signerInfo`/…). Per-step verdict (the agreed rule — title text taken **verbatim** from the form, this sweep only changes *structure*):
- **ok** (609) — already has a dedicated step-title panel, **or a child draw already renders the title** (a `titledraw`/`textdraw` whose `_value` text *exactly* equals the step title) → **skip** (synthesizing would duplicate the visible heading). **Only a drawn `_value` counts as "rendered" — a panel's `jcr:title` does NOT render as a visible heading** (validated on AAAI `PN_Agreement`, AACB `PN_Parte1`, `PN_StpSectionIiSectionTitle` — all panels whose `jcr:title` matched the step title but showed no heading; those steps need synth, not skip).
- **wrap:move** (77) — a `headingLevel="2"` draw exists but isn't wrapped → **move that draw intact** to the top of the step and wrap it; remove the step's own `jcr:title`. Translation-safe (validated: AAAM move kept its German title).
- **wrap:synth** (274) — no draw renders the title → synthesize a draw from the step's `jcr:title` at the top; remove the step's `jcr:title`. Covers steps whose title only lived in a panel `jcr:title` (AAAI, AACB sections, AAAL — all validated single after).
- **strip:double** (26) — already wrapped (a `headingLevel="2"` draw whose text *equals* the step's own `jcr:title`) **and** the step still carries that `jcr:title` → AEM renders the heading **twice on screen** (`dorExcludeTitle` only hides it in the DoR/PDF, not the form) → **strip the redundant step `jcr:title`** (no insert). Validated: AACB first two showed twice, single after.
- **wrap:promote** (45) — no h2 draw and no step `jcr:title`, **but the step LEADS with a `titledraw`** (its real title rendered at the wrong heading level: `h3`/`h4`/none) → promote that draw to a proper step title: set `headingLevel="2"` + `css="stepTitle"`. **If it's already inside a dedicated step-title panel** (17 of 45) → promote **in place** (no new panel — else double-wrap); **otherwise** (28 of 45) → move it intact to the top and wrap it in `{name}Title`. A leading *textdraw* (body text) or no draw → stays titleless. Validated: AACT (in-place, single panel), AAEU "Checkliste"/AAFA "Finanzdienstleister" (move).
- **flag:trans** (7) — `jcr:title` is translation-linked (path-keyed) and no draw renders it → auto-wrap + flag "update translations".
- **titleless** (31) — no title anywhere (no h2 draw, no `jcr:title`, and the leading drawn element is a textdraw/body or absent) → **skip**.

Rules that made the fix safe (all from AEM validation): **remove the step's own `jcr:title`** when wrapping (else AEM renders it a second time), **a panel `jcr:title` never renders a visible heading** (so only a matching drawn `_value` blocks a synth — everything else gets a proper title), and **strip the step `jcr:title` when a wrapped draw already shows the same text** (`strip:double`). Translations are text-keyed (`dictionary/<lang>.xml`, `sling:key="fd_<text>"`), so keeping title text verbatim preserves them.

Detection keys on a `headingLevel="2"` title-draw, **not** `css="stepTitle"` (that marker also sits on wrapper panels / `textdraw`s and is absent on ~415 — it both over- and under-matches). **182 forms / 422 auto-fixes (77 move + 274 synth + 26 strip + 45 promote) / 7 flags** of 286 packages.
**Detect:** script: `find_step_title_panel.py`  _(emits `wrap[]` {form,step,source:move|synth|strip|promote,title} and `flag[]` {…,reason}; that is the review surface — verify it before building/running the fix)_
**Fix:** script: `set_step_title_panel.py` _(byte-offset structural surgery: insert a `{name}Title` panel as the step's first child; `move` = relocate the existing draw intact (preserves translations), `synth` = build a draw from `jcr:title`, `strip` = remove the redundant step `jcr:title` (no insert), `promote` = a leading titledraw at the wrong heading level → set `headingLevel=2`+`css=stepTitle` (in place if already in a title panel, else move+wrap); all also remove the step's own `jcr:title` and set `dorExcludeTitle="true"` to match the engine. Auto-fixes `move`+`synth`+`strip`+`promote`+`flag:trans`; skips `ok` and `titleless`. Mirrors the detector's skip rules. Prints `{inner, wrapped:[{step,source}], ok}`.)_
**Verify:** the fix script's per-step `ok` (well-formed result + every targeted step now has a first-child step-title panel, or had its redundant title stripped) + a pilot **visual spot-check** (title shows once, correctly). The 2 `flag:trans` steps are auto-wrapped too but **listed in the issue table as "update translations"** — the owner tops up their translations after rollout.
**Max changed lines:** omit — additive structural insert; gate on removed==0 (move adds/relocates, never deletes content) + the fix's per-step `ok`.
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** manual (engine-confirmed for structure only)
**Tracking:** #18
**Status:** **swept 2026-06-30 (182/182 fixed + deployed, PR #19 merged).** Detector + fixer validated in AEM on AAAM (move, translation held), AAAN (synth), AACB (strip:double + 26 section synths), AAAI/AAAL (synth, no double), AACT/AAEU/AAFA (promote). Three rule corrections during the pilot: added `strip:double` (AACB's first two doubled); tightened `renders_title_elsewhere` to **draw `_value` only** (a panel `jcr:title` doesn't render → titles living only in a panel `jcr:title` are now synthed); added `wrap:promote` (titleless step leading with a titledraw at the wrong heading level → promote to h2, in place if already in a title panel else move+wrap). The 7 `flag:trans` steps (4 forms) were resolved **not-actionable** — all are `jcr:language="en"` with orphaned dictionaries (no live second locale), so the English synth titles are correct. 31 steps remain genuinely titleless. One edge: `contentPanel_forInternalBankUseOnly` (1 form) is a direct root child that looks like content, not a step.

**Dependency:** this is the **prerequisite** the jump-to-field sweep (#12) is deferred behind — jump-to-field's button goes on the step-title draw, which must first be correctly wrapped. **Land #18 before re-piloting #12.** Also overlaps banking (#10) / dor-template (#14) on the same `_merged.zip`s → sequence rollouts (merge one, rebase + `--continue` the rest; the fix re-derives each zip from base, so idempotent).

---

## PROBLEM-panel-type-ubs — Every panel must be the UBS custom panel
**Symptom:** a panel uses the **default AEM panel** (`sling:resourceType="fd/af/components/panel"`) instead of the **UBS custom panel** (`ajila-forms-customers/ajila-forms-ubs/components/controls/panel`). The default panel lacks the UBS authoring sections (notably the **Summary** tab that carries `jumpToFieldButtonVisible`), so those options can't be set/rendered. **The rule is unconditional: every occurrence of `fd/af/components/panel` becomes the UBS custom panel, whatever node carries it — no exceptions** (owner directive 2026-08-24, reaffirmed: "the sweep should replace ALL aem panels with ubs panels"). That covers wizard panels, step-title panels, repeatable **wrappers** (owner directive 2026-07-20), **fragment-reference panels (`fragRef`)** — which the retired [PROBLEM-fragment-panel-aem](#) / #24 used to force the other way — and **container nodes with no `guideNodeClass`**. Corpus at widening: **1,668** occurrences across **304 forms** = 1,666 on `guideNodeClass="guidePanel"` nodes (all of them fragment panels; the non-fragment scope was already clean) + **2 `<items>` container nodes** (BBDY_033, BBDZ_019 — the `items` of a `PN_Definitions_Accordion` panel, where a *layout* normally sits; one hand-authored source copied twice). Verified against a raw quote-aware string scan of every `.xml` in all 304 packages: 1,668 occurrences, all in the main form `.content.xml`, all exact `sling:resourceType` values — so after rollout the string is gone from the corpus. Not in scope, because they are **different components**, not the default AEM panel: 44 `ajila-forms-ubs/components/controls/letterhead` (already a UBS component) and 308 `fd/af/components/rootPanel` — the root-panel component has **no UBS equivalent** (the only UBS panel types in the corpus are `controls/panel` and the `layouts/panel/wizard` *layout*). 4 of those 308 are `<items>` containers typed as the root component — the same container-typed-as-a-component oddity as the two above, reported as `container_rootpanel` and left alone pending a decision.
**Detect:** script: `find_panel_type.py`  _(counts/lists **every node** with `sling:resourceType="fd/af/components/panel"`, any node kind; `affected` = forms with ≥1. `swap_fragment` / `swap_container` are informational subsets of `swap`, `other` the letterhead panels, `container_rootpanel` the 4 out-of-scope rootPanel-typed containers)_
**Fix:** script: `set_panel_type.py` _(byte-offset splice: change only the `sling:resourceType` value `fd/af/components/panel` → `ajila-forms-customers/ajila-forms-ubs/components/controls/panel`, on every node carrying it — no node-kind filter. No other resourceType is matched, so `rootPanel`, `letterhead` and every layout are untouched; prints `{inner, swapped, swapped_fragment, swapped_container, ok}`)_
**Verify:** the fix script's `ok` (well-formed) + a re-scan showing **0** occurrences of `fd/af/components/panel` in the package (raw string scan, not just guidePanel nodes) + a pilot **visual spot-check** (panels render identically on the UBS component). The pilot must include a form with a fragment panel, one with a `letterhead` panel, and one of the two `<items>`-container forms — the fragment must render and still resolve its `fragRef`; the letterhead must be unchanged; the accordion whose `items` was retyped must still open and hold its children.
**Max changed lines:** omit — one attribute value changed per swapped panel (no adds/removes); gate on the fix's `ok` + re-scan.
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** owner directive 2026-08-24 (blanket UBS panel). Overrides the engine on both sub-cases: `repeatable.xml` emits the repeatable outer wrapper as the default AEM panel, and `fragment.xml` emits fragment panels as the default AEM panel — the owner does not want either in the deployed forms.
**Tracking:** #137 _(widened rollout; the original non-fragment rollout was #20, closed)_
**Status:** **swept 2026-08-24 (304/304 fixed + deployed, PR #138)** — **1,668** occurrences of `fd/af/components/panel` replaced with the UBS custom panel: 1,666 on `guideNodeClass="guidePanel"` nodes (all fragment panels) + 2 `<items>` containers (BBDY_033, BBDZ_019). Every batch 0-flagged with `delta`+`diff`+`verify`+`deploy` ok. **Verified by a raw quote-aware string scan of every `.xml` in all 304 packages: 0 occurrences left** — the string is gone from the corpus, not merely absent from guidePanel nodes; the detector agrees (0 affected, 10,407 UBS panels). Untouched as different components: 44 `letterhead`, 308 `fd/af/components/rootPanel` (no UBS equivalent exists), of which 4 are `<items>` containers typed as the root component — reported as `container_rootpanel`, still open. Landed with #88's canonical flip (see Dependency); full-corpus guard green on all 30 enrolled rules. Rollout ran in 8 chunks of ≤40 forms rather than one `--continue`, since a run commits only at its end. **Merge sequencing:** 224 of the 304 forms are also committed on 8 other in-flight sweep branches, so those conflict at merge (LFS, no 3-way) — merge this, then rebase and re-run the others; the fix re-derives each zip from base, so it is idempotent.
**History:** swept 2026-07-01 for the *non-fragment* scope only (258/258 fixed + deployed, PR #21 merged) — 2,798 non-fragment default-AEM panels → UBS; 666 fragment panels left untouched then (verified byte-identical on AACR: 11 default + 4 UBS fragments). Unblocked #12 (jump-to-field). **Bidirectional experiment reverted 2026-07-20:** a rerun (PR #62, since reverted) had made the detector/fixer bidirectional and flipped 310 non-fragment repeatable *wrappers* from UBS → default AEM to match `repeatable.xml`; on AEM review the owner directed that all non-fragment panels stay UBS, the scripts were restored to one-way `default → UBS`, and the 310 wrappers across 161 forms were swapped back and redeployed (PR #63, 2026-07-20 — 161/161, 0 flagged, `verify:ok`+`deploy:ok`). **Widened 2026-08-24:** the fragment carve-out was dropped and #24 (which had pushed 629 fragment panels the other way, PR #25) retired, making this the single panel-type rule.

## PROBLEM-fragment-panel-aem — RETIRED / DISABLED (inverted by #20)
**Do not run this entry.** It is kept for the record only. Its rule — fragment-reference panels (`fragRef` guidePanels) must be the **default AEM panel** — was **inverted by owner directive 2026-08-24**: fragment panels are now UBS custom panels like every other panel, and the single rule that says so is [PROBLEM-panel-type-ubs](#) / #20. Running the fix below would undo #20, so both scripts (`find_fragment_aem.py`, `set_fragment_aem.py`) now **hard-fail** with a pointer to #20 instead of doing anything.
**Symptom (historical):** fragment-reference panels used the UBS custom panel instead of the default AEM panel the engine emits for fragments. Corpus at the time: **629** UBS-type fragment panels across **202 forms** (666 already default), including the hidden `formmetadata` fragment.
**Detect:** _retired — `find_fragment_aem.py` hard-fails; use `find_panel_type.py` (#20), which now covers fragment panels._
**Fix:** _retired — `set_fragment_aem.py` hard-fails; use `set_panel_type.py` (#20)._
**Runs automatically:** no — the tooling ignores this entry entirely.
**Origin:** engine-diff (the engine's `fragment.xml` emits fragment panels as the default AEM panel — **overridden** by the owner's blanket-UBS directive, same as `repeatable.xml` was on 2026-07-20).
**Tracking:** #24
**Status:** **retired 2026-08-24 — superseded by [PROBLEM-panel-type-ubs](#) / #20; not enforced, do not re-run.** Previously rolled out 2026-07-02 (202/202 fixed + deployed, PR #25 merged) — 629 UBS fragment panels → default AEM, engine-confirmed at the time by a fresh AAAE conversion. #20's widened rollout reverses exactly that population.

## PROBLEM-nav-button-order — Wizard toolbar navigation buttons in the wrong order
**Symptom:** the toolbar's core nav buttons must be in the engine order `nextitemnav` (Next) → `submit` (Submit) → `previtemnav` (Back), with any extra buttons (preview / `fwbSaveProgress` / custom preview / tertiary) after. 66 forms deviate — mostly Back-first (`previtemnav, nextitemnav, submit`), 4 Submit-first. Engine-confirmed via fresh conversions (AAAN, AAAE): order is Next, Submit, Back, Preview.
**Detect:** script: `find_nav_order.py`  _(compares the relative order of the three core buttons in the toolbar; affected if all three present but not Next→Submit→Back)_
**Fix:** script: `set_nav_order.py` _(byte-offset element relocation: moves the three core buttons to the front in engine order, keeps every other toolbar child in its existing relative order; prints `{order_before, order_after, ok}`)_
**Verify:** the fix's `ok` (well-formed) + re-scan showing 0 wrong + a pilot visual check (button row renders in the right order).
**Max changed lines:** omit — structural reorder; gate on `ok` + re-scan.
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** engine-diff (engine emits Next, Submit, Back, Preview)
**Tracking:** #27
**Status:** **swept 2026-07-02 (66/66 fixed + deployed, PR #28 merged)** — toolbar reordered to engine order Next→Submit→Back, trailing buttons kept.

## PROBLEM-banking-relationship-wrap — Wrap the first-page banking panel in a PN_BR exclusion panel
**Symptom:** the **first-page** banking-relationship fragment panel (`guideNodeClass="guidePanel"` with a `fragRef` ending in `affrg_BankingRelationship1`) must be wrapped in a dedicated UBS custom panel named `PN_BR` carrying `summaryExclusion="true"` + `dorExclusion="true"`, so the whole banking subtree is excluded from the summary and the DoR. This is an established hand-authored convention — **21 forms already ship the `PN_BR` wrapper** (UBS custom panel, both exclusions, banking fragment as sole child). The fragment panel already carries `dorExclusion="true"` from [PROBLEM-banking-relationship-fragment](#) / #10; this sweep adds the *summary* exclusion via the wrapper and gives the DoR exclusion its correct structural home (a wrapper isolates the whole subtree, which flags on the fragment panel alone may not do in the rendered summary). **First page only** — banking panels on later wizard steps are left alone (9 forms are later-page-only; no form has banking on both page 1 and a later page).
**The message a person sees says what to do, and what it costs (owner request 2026-09-01).** The
detector now emits a `violations[]` of `{form, name, reason}`, which is the shape
`check_regressions.describe_form` reads — the older `forms` key is one of the names that function
deliberately skips, so until this was added an affected form showed only "(affected — see detector
output)" in the intake app. The reason states that `PN_BR` holds the banking-relationship fragment
and **may also hold a complementary text beside it** (the "UBS Europe SE, Succursale Italia" line),
and that **everything inside `PN_BR` is excluded from the summary AND from the DoR**, so such a text
renders on screen only and will not appear in either.
**Detect:** script: `find_banking_wrap.py`  _(finds first-page banking fragRef panels; affected = forms with ≥1 unwrapped one; already-`PN_BR` (name, or a UBS panel with both exclusions + sole child) and later-page banking are skipped — idempotent for rebase + --continue)_
**Fix:** script: `set_banking_wrap.py`  _(byte-offset wrap surgery mirroring #18: moves the fragment panel **intact** into a new `PN_BR` UBS custom panel with summaryExclusion+dorExclusion; multiple panels spliced back-to-front; prints `{inner, wrapped, skipped_wrapped, skipped_later, ok}`)_
**Verify:** the fix's `ok` (well-formed) + a re-scan showing 0 unwrapped first-page banking panels remain + a pilot **visual spot-check** (banking section no longer appears in the summary; the form itself renders unchanged).
**Max changed lines:** omit — additive structural wrap; gate on the fix's `ok` + re-scan.
**Runs automatically:** no
**Origin:** manual (candidate engine-fix — the engine emits the exclusion flags *on* the fragment panel in `profiles/ubs/aem/fragment.xml`, which may not exclude the rendered subtree from the summary; the `PN_BR` wrapper is the reliable structure the owner hand-authored on 21 forms).
**Tracking:** #29
**Status:** **swept 2026-07-02 (232/232 fixed + deployed, PR #30 merged)** — first-page banking fragment wrapped in a `PN_BR` UBS panel with summaryExclusion+dorExclusion; 22 already had `PN_BR`, 9 later-page-only left untouched. `validate_delta` was updated to ignore benign ancestor re-paths (a wrap/move re-paths a pre-existing violation deeper — one-in/one-out, net-zero), so the rollout ran 0-flagged.

**`FormMetadata` was being counted as the first page (Marco's summary-page report, 2026-08-21).** "First page" was read as step index 0. But [PROBLEM-formmetadata-step](#) / #100 requires the `FormMetadata` fragment as the form's **first node** and that fragment is step-shaped, so index 0 has been *that fragment* in **all 282 forms that carry a banking panel** — every genuinely-first-page banking panel has been out of this rule's scope ever since the metadata sweep landed, which is why Marco's forms kept showing the banking block in the summary. `steps()` / `first_step()` now skip the `FormMetadata` step by name. **277 forms come back into scope; 11 are still unwrapped** and are the work this uncovers: AAAE_019, AABJ_019, AAFN_033, AAKW_019, AALU_019, AAOV_033, BAJS_033, BBFI_033, BBHI_019, BBPT_019, BBQG_033. The other 266 already ship a `PN_BR` and stay skipped.

**THE WRAPPER MAY HOLD MORE THAN THE FRAGMENT (owner directive 2026-09-01).** `is_wrapped` required the
banking fragment to be the wrapper's **sole child**, so a correct hand-authored `PN_BR` stopped reading
as wrapped the moment anything was put beside the fragment — which the owner does on purpose: Italy
forms put a complementary text (the "UBS Europe SE, Succursale Italia" line) inside `PN_BR` so it
renders directly under the banking block. The test is now: a UBS panel carrying **both** exclusions
that either is NAMED `PN_BR` or holds the fragment as its sole child. The exclusion VALUES are still
required, so a wrapper that lost a flag is still flagged; the name is safe to trust because
`set_banking_wrap.py` names every wrapper it creates `PN_BR`, which keeps a rebase + `--continue`
idempotent, and the sole-child branch stays for hand-authored wrappers under another name (AACB_033).
Anything else inside the wrapper inherits its exclusions and is therefore out of the summary and the
DoR too — the owner confirmed that is intended for this text, which is screen-only like the banking
block. **Measured: affected 11 -> 7.** The four that come off are genuine `PN_BR` wrappers holding one
extra component: AABJ_019 (`TXT_Asset_Link_ID`), AAFN_033 (`ST_Sottotitolo` — "Art. 46 D.P.R. 28
dicembre 2000, n. 445"), AAKW_019 (`TXT_CustodyAccount`), AALU_019 (`TXT_DepotNr`). Remaining: AAAE_019
AAOV_033 BAJS_033 BBFI_033 BBHI_019 BBPT_019 BBQG_033.

**The fixer had its own second copy of `is_wrapped`, and the two disagreed in BOTH directions.** This
file's copy returned True on the `PN_BR` name alone, *before* checking the exclusions, so a wrapper
that had lost a flag was skipped by the fixer and flagged by the detector for ever; the detector's copy
demanded the sole child, so a `PN_BR` with the Succursale text was reported affected on every run.
`set_banking_wrap.py` now imports `find_banking_wrap.is_wrapped` (duck-typed on
`.tag`/`.attrs`/`.children`/`.parent`, so it accepts the fixer's positioned nodes unchanged).

**Downstream:** the `PN_BR` wrapper this sweep creates must also carry `css="ubs-margin-20"` → [PROBLEM-banking-relationship-margin](#). `set_banking_wrap.py` emits no `css`, so every wrapper it made is affected.

## PROBLEM-footnote-modernize — Footnotes → canonical engine (AAEP) model
**Symptom:** footnotes must use the engine/AAEP model — an inline reference `<span data-af-footnote-id="ID"><sup>#</sup></span>` in the rich value, a hidden description `<p id="ID" class="footnoteDescription" style="display: none;"><span class="footnoteDescText">TEXT</span></p>` right after it, and one `guidefootnoteplaceholder` (`guideNodeClass="guideFootnotePlaceHolder"`) per page where footnotes render. Many deployed forms instead use a **legacy accordion** (`css="ubsAccordionFootnote"` panel holding the footnote texts, with raw `<sup>N</sup>` / unicode `¹²³` markers in rich `_value`s **and** choice `options` labels), or carry modern refs but are **missing the placeholder**, or have a **stray placeholder** with no refs. **35 of 286 packages** are safely automatable (32 accordion + 2 add-placeholder + 1 stray). 7 more need per-form judgment and are **out of scope** (accordion holding non-footnote prose: AAPC/AAXC; footnote text with no reference: AAPQ; orphan markers with no recoverable text: AACY/AAFC/AAQG/ABFG).
**Detect:** script: `find_footnote_noncanonical.py`  _(returns only auto-safe forms: accordion forms where **every** body marker — sup or unicode, in `_value` or `options` — resolves to a footnote text extractable from the accordion, plus modern-refs-missing-placeholder and stray-placeholder; excludes misused/unresolved/orphan as manual. Idempotent: a converted form matches none of the shapes.)_
**Fix:** script: `set_footnote_modern.py`  _(escaped-domain byte-offset surgery: extract marker→text from each accordion, rewrite every marker to inline ref + hidden desc (in `_value`s and per-option `options` labels, setting `textIsRich`), replace each accordion **in place** with a placeholder; add/remove placeholder for the other two modes. Ports the engine's ID scheme `base64("0."+marker)`−`=+/` and exact span/desc strings. Prints `{mode, footnotes, refs, placeholders, ok, flags}`; **hard self-check** sets `ok:false` on any leftover resolvable marker, remaining accordion, or malformed output.)_
**Verify:** must-not: `ubsAccordionFootnote`  _(+ the fix's `ok` (well-formed, no leftover marker, accordion gone, ref produced) + a pilot **visual check**: footnote superscripts render as `#` linking to the description, the placeholder shows the footnotes, and option-label footnotes render as rich text not literal markup)_
**Max changed lines:** omit — structural rewrite of varying size; gate on the fix's `ok` + must-not + delta.
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** manual (owner directive — engine `profiles/ubs/aem/footnote*.xml` + `converter.rs` is the confirmed-correct reference; deployed exemplars AAEP and AACR/BATI)
**Tracking:** #53
**Status:** swept 2026-07-20 (35/35 automatable fixed + deployed, PR #54) — pilot (6, all shapes incl. unicode-in-options + rich lists) reviewed OK, then --continue for the remaining 29; all 0-flagged with delta passing (engine MCP: no new schema violations). **7 forms deliberately out of scope** (manual): AAPC/AAXC (accordion holds non-footnote prose), AAPQ (footnote text, no reference), AACY/AAFC/AAQG/ABFG (orphan markers) — these remain by design; the detector returns only auto-safe shapes, so it reports 0 on master. Marked swept 2026-07-20 after `check_regressions.py` confirmed 0 affected.

---

## PROBLEM-infobox-dor-copy — Infobox: DoR-excluded on screen + a DoR-only copy on the last page
**Symptom:** a form carrying an **infobox** (the `affrg_italy_infobox` fragment) must (1) keep every **on-screen** infobox panel out of the Document of Record — `dorExclusion="true"` **and** `summaryExclusion="true"` on the panel bearing the fragment reference, because the UBS DoR is rendered by Redacto from the *summary* HTML and `dorExclusion` alone hides nothing (see [PROBLEM-dor-exclusion-implies-summary](#) / #151) — and (2) carry a **DoR-only copy of that same infobox** as the **last item of its last content step**: a second panel referencing `affrg_italy_infobox`, `visible="{Boolean}false"` **plus `alwaysInPdf="true"` and `summaryExclusion="true"`**, so the infobox text renders at the bottom of the last page of the DoR and nowhere on screen. **`alwaysInPdf` is what makes the copy exist at all (correction 2026-08-27, see History):** the UBS DoR is Redacto rendering the *summary* data, and the summary builder (`ajila-forms-ubs/clientlibs/common/js/newcontext/summary-backend-creator.js`, `pushData`) gates every node as `summaryExclusion -> alwaysInPdf ? emit with class "pdf-only" : return` / `visible===false -> showIfHidden ? emit : alwaysInPdf ? "pdf-only" : return`. `pdf-only` is hidden by the screen theme only (`.summary .pdf-only{display:none}` in ebanking.less) and does reach the PDF; `dorExclusion` is **not read on this path at all**. A copy that is merely `visible="{Boolean}false"` is therefore dropped before Redacto sees it and renders nowhere — the earlier reading ("hidden still reaches the DoR because [PROBLEM-dor-exclude-hidden](#) / #8 removed `excludeFromDoRIfHidden` corpus-wide") holds for Adobe's *native* DoR, not for the Redacto one UBS renders. **The two roles are told apart by `visible`:** `visible` absent/true = screen panel, requirement (1) applies and `alwaysInPdf` must be absent; `visible` false = the DoR copy, which needs the `alwaysInPdf`+`summaryExclusion` pair and must never carry `dorExclusion`. Reference shape: the hand-built `AAOS_033` package (2026-08-27) and the corpus's own DoR-only blocks (`PN_InternalBankUseOnly`, that trio since April 2026). **Corpus 2026-08-26: 28 of 304 packages carry an infobox, all `_033`; all 28 already satisfy (1)** (from the earlier rollout plus #152), **0 satisfy (2)**. Shape is uniform: one single fragment path (`/content/dam/formsanddocuments/afforms_italy_fragmentlib/affrg_italy_infobox`, 28/28), UBS custom panel with `layout`+`items` children (28/28), `guideNodeClass="guidePanel"`, `name="PN_ItalyInfobox"` (27) / `PN_InfoBox` (1, BAWZ_033), nested 2–3 levels inside the *first* content step. `affrg_italy_infobox` is the **only** infobox fragment in any UBS fragment library (checked all four — ch / germany / italy / ubs) and the only one referenced in the corpus, so the rule is Italy-scoped by nature, not by choice. The inline `controls/infobox` **component** (25 instances in 22 forms, all already `dorExclusion`+`summaryExclusion`) is **out of scope** — owner decision 2026-08-26. The Italy **footnote fragment** (`affrg_italy_footnote`, the same disclaimer in a hidden fragment) sits on the last content step of all 28 today; the owner decided to **leave every one in place**, so the detector reports it (`footnote_on_last`) for review but the rule neither requires nor removes it.
**Detect:** script: `find_infobox_dor_copy.py`  _(affected = an on-screen infobox panel missing either exclusion flag, no DoR-only copy on the last content step, or a copy that is there but lacks `alwaysInPdf`/`summaryExclusion` and so renders nowhere. Anything needing a person — a copy carrying `dorExclusion`, an on-screen panel carrying `alwaysInPdf`, a form whose last content step cannot be identified — lands in `manual`, never in `affected`, so the rule can reach 0 and the CI guard stays honest. "Last content step" = the last panel under `rootPanel > items` that is not wizard plumbing — matched by name **and** by content signature (`controls/summary`, `actions/previewbutton`, the `formmetadata` fragRef), because names alone are not dependable across the two lineages. Presence for (2) is tested over the **whole last-step subtree**, so a form that already has the copy is never given a second one. `--report` prints the review table (including which forms also still carry the footnote fragment). Forms with no infobox are never affected; idempotent.)_
**Fix:** script: `set_infobox_dor_copy.py`  _(three passes, all through `set_fragment_panel.transform` — the same code that puts `dorExclusion` on the banking-relationship panel in #10: (1) both exclusion flags on the **on-screen** panels, **skipping any panel with `visible` false** via its `skip_attrs` hook; (2) the mirror image via the new `only_attrs` hook — `alwaysInPdf="true"`+`summaryExclusion="true"` on every **hidden** infobox panel, which is what repairs the 28 inert copies from the first rollout in place; then the tree is **re-parsed against the edited bytes** and (3) if the last content step holds no hidden infobox panel at any depth, the canonical DoR-only panel is appended as its **last item**. The `skip_attrs`/`only_attrs` split is what stops the two shapes bleeding into each other; without it the fix would destroy its own output on the second run. The inserted node copies the corpus's uniform infobox shape with the engine `fragment.xml` children (`layout` incl. `dorNumCols`, `items` **carrying** `sling:resourceType`, empty `fd:rules`/`fd:scripts`) and the UBS panel resourceType (#137 overrides the engine there); tag and name (`PN_ItalyInfoboxDoR`) are made unique against the form. An empty `<items …/>` on the last step is expanded rather than spliced into — `element_span` of a self-closed tag would otherwise send the backwards `</` search into an unrelated element. Prints `{infoboxes, screen_panels, attrs_added, attrs_updated, skipped_hidden, existing_copies, copy_attrs_added, copies_added, last_step, footnote_on_last, ok, flags}`; **hard self-check** sets `ok:false` unless the output is well-formed **and** the detector's `classify` then reports the form clean on both counts.)_
**Verify:** _no regex gate — the fix's own `ok` plus a detector re-scan reporting 0 affected._  _(A file-level grep cannot tell **which** panel carries `dorExclusion` and which is the hidden copy, and `dorExclusion="true"` already appears in every one of these forms, so such a gate would pass unconditionally. Note `run_sweep` applies a `must` list only when the fix reports no `ok`, so a regex here would be dead weight anyway.)_ Plus a pilot **visual check of the generated DoR** on the instance that renders DoRs (192.168.64.3): the disclaimer appears at the bottom of the last page and **not** inline where the on-screen infobox sits — and a check of the **rendered form**: no infobox on the last page on screen, and none on the summary step. **This check is the whole rule** — the first rollout passed every structural gate with copies that render nothing, so a structural re-scan is necessary and not sufficient. Watch for the disclaimer appearing **twice** in the DoR (the footnote fragment is deliberately left in place; the hand-built AAOS_033 reference *replaced* it with the copy instead) — if it does, that is a decision for the owner, not a fixer change.
**Max changed lines:** 25  _(purely additive; **+2/-0** on all 28 for the 2026-08-27 attribute repair (the copy is already in place, it just gains `alwaysInPdf`+`summaryExclusion`), +24/-0 for a form that still needs the panel appended — measured on AAOS_033/BBFI_033/AAPC_033/AAPR_033/BAWZ_033/AAEA_033. A form over 25 means the attribute half fired too — worth a look, not a failure.)_
**Blocker to confirm on the pilot (not fixable from this repo):** the fragment's **own** inner infobox component carries `dorExclusion="true"` inside `affrg_italy_infobox/.content.xml` (engine `profiles/ubs/aem/fragments/afforms_italy_fragmentlib/`). Reading the summary builder (see Symptom) **downgrades this to unlikely**: `dorExclusion` is not consulted on the Redacto path, so the inner component should still be emitted. It would bite only if a form's DoR is ever rendered from the XDP instead. If the pilot shows nothing at the bottom of the last page even with `alwaysInPdf` set, that flag in the fragment library is the next suspect, and lifting it is a UBS/engine-side task.
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-08-26: drop the footnote-fragment half; copy the infobox itself to the last page at the last position, analogous to the footnote element, and show that copy only in the DoR. Reference for the panel shape: the deployed infobox panels (28/28 identical) + engine `fragment.xml`.)
**Tracking:** #140
**Ordering:** **after** [PROBLEM-dor-exclude-hidden](#) / #8 (rolled out 2026-06-23 — without it a hidden panel is dropped from the DoR and the copy is pointless) and after [PROBLEM-dor-exclusion-implies-summary](#) / #151 (requirement (1) is its target shape). No conflict with #151 in the other direction: its detector selects `dorExclusion="true"` open tags and the DoR copy carries none, so it never adds `summaryExclusion` to the copy. [PROBLEM-naming-conventions](#) / #64 exempts any node with a `fragRef`, so `PN_ItalyInfoboxDoR` is safe either way. Appending into a step that previously held **only a title panel** could newly trip [PROBLEM-jump-to-field-button](#) / #12 — not reachable today: all 28 last content steps already hold content (the footnote fragment at minimum). AAPC_033 is the form to watch, as it was for the earlier shape.
**Unit tests:** `.claude/scripts/tests/test_infobox_dor_copy.py` (20 cases, plain asserts, no pytest) — role split by `visible`, copy found at any depth, copy on an earlier step not counted, **a copy without `alwaysInPdf` reported as affected (the shape the first rollout deployed) and repaired in place rather than duplicated**, an on-screen panel carrying `alwaysInPdf` flagged rather than "fixed", a copy carrying `dorExclusion` flagged rather than "fixed", idempotency including "the fix never excludes its own copy", self-closed `items` expansion, and the byte-identical no-op on a form without an infobox. Writing them surfaced a **silent bug in `set_fragment_panel.transform`**: when `fragRef` was not on its own indented line the attribute insert no-opped while still counting as `attrs_added`. It now delegates to `set_attr_on.ensure_attr` (one inserter for the repo, single-line tags included). Harmless in the corpus (all fragRef panels are multi-line) but it would have lied on a hand-authored form.
**History:** **correction 2026-08-27 — the first rollout shipped 28 inert copies.** The owner supplied a hand-built `AAOS_033` package as the reference; its DoR-only infobox carries `alwaysInPdf="true"` + `summaryExclusion="true"` + `visible="{Boolean}false"` (no `dorExclusion`), while the 28 deployed copies carried `visible="{Boolean}false"` and nothing else. Reading the summary builder settled which is right (see Symptom): the hidden copy is dropped from the summary data before Redacto sees it, so **requirement (2) was satisfied structurally and achieved nothing** in every one of the 28 forms. It slipped through because the DoR was never rendered during pilot or rollout (the Status line said so at the time) and because every structural gate — the fix's `ok`, the detector re-scan, `delta`, `check_regressions` — can only see the node, not whether it renders. The repair is `alwaysInPdf`+`summaryExclusion` on the existing copy (+2/-0 per form); the reference's *placement* was deliberately **not** copied, because it puts the copy inside a hidden `PN_InternalBankUseOnly` container that only **2 of the 28** forms have (the other footnote parents are 19× `PN_Signature`, 4× `PN_Firma`, and one each of `PN_Collocamento`, `PN_FirmaE`, `PN_Signatures_title`), so a standalone panel as the last item of the last step stays the corpus-wide shape. The reference also **removed** the Italy footnote fragment from that container and put the infobox copy in its slot, which is the owner's earlier "leave every footnote in place" decision pointing the other way — left in place here, flagged on the Verify line. Earlier history: this rule previously required the Italy **footnote fragment** on the last content step instead of the infobox copy (slug `PROBLEM-infobox-dor-footnote`, scripts `infobox_footnote.py` / `find_infobox_footnote.py` / `set_infobox_footnote.py`, all renamed 2026-08-26). That shape was rolled out 2026-08-24 (28/28 fixed + deployed, 0 flagged, PR #141): piloted 6, then **2 `--only` chunks of 10** (run_sweep commits once at the end of a run, so a run dying late loses every form it had already deployed); all 28 read back out of the live AEM, `check_regressions.py` 30/30 clean. Requirement (1) was `dorExclusion` only back then ("the directive covers the DoR only"); #152 has since made `summaryExclusion` mandatory everywhere, which is why (1) now asks for both. Two course corrections from that rollout, both worth remembering: the **first** pilot built the requirement against Adobe's `guidefootnoteplaceholder` component (wrong element — it renders only the stub "The footnotes will appear here"; reverted), and the synthesised `items` node initially omitted `sling:resourceType`, copying debt 54 of 56 deployed panels carry (the delta gate refused it; the engine template was the right reference). Fragments the earlier sweep appended by hand-equivalent: **BBFI_033** (none anywhere → appended to `PN_Firma`) and **AAPC_033** (had one on `PN_Firm/PN_Signature` but not on its last content step `PN_Collocamento`). Both stay — see the owner decision above. **AAPC_033 still carries two footnote fragments**; removing one is a judgment call, and its planned restructure ("Collocamento fuori sede… should be a single accordion starting on the next page") will change its page order, so **re-check AAPC_033 after that lands**.
**Pilot (2026-08-26, 6/28, PR #155):** a deliberately shape-diverse batch instead of the default first six: **AAOS_033** (the form the DoR report came from), **BAWZ_033** (the one `PN_InfoBox` name), **AAPC_033** (two footnote fragments, restructure pending), **BBFI_033** (footnote appended by the earlier rollout), **AAPR_033** (odd last-step name `PN_InCasoDiRacco_MainSection`), **AAEA_033** (vanilla). Every form `delta ok` `diff ok` `verify ok` `deploy ok`, +24/-0 each. **Read back out of the live JCR** with bounded-depth node GETs (not install return codes): all 6 carry the copy with `fragRef` = the infobox fragment, `visible=false`, **neither** exclusion flag, as the **last child** of the last content step's `items`, and the on-screen panel still carries both flags. The **rendered form** (AAOS_033, `afAcceptLang=it`, HTTP 200) resolves the copy's fragment — `panel_italyinfobox_dor → PN_ItalyInfobox → ITXT_Italy` with the disclaimer text and `"visible":false` in the client model — so the node is live and hidden on screen. **The DoR itself is still unchecked:** 192.168.64.3, the instance that renders DoRs, was unreachable during the pilot (the `.env` Parallels instance cannot render one). What the rendered model does show narrows the blocker above to a **testable prediction**: the copy's inner `ITXT_Italy` carries `dorExclusion="true"` from the fragment but **no** `summaryExclusion`, and `dorExclusion` alone removes nothing from the Redacto DoR — so the copy plausibly *does* render, and the fragment may not need changing after all. Confirm on the DoR-rendering instance before `--continue`. **Also new:** each fixed form now carries the fragment-internal names `PN_ItalyInfobox` / `ITXT_Italy` **twice** (both come from the fragment, so this is inherent to referencing it twice); a corpus scan found **no** rule or script referencing either name, so nothing breaks today, but a future name-based lookup must not assume uniqueness. **Human review still open** — last page shows no infobox on screen, the summary step does not list it, and the DoR shows the disclaimer **once** at the bottom of the last page (watch for twice: the footnote fragment deliberately stays).
**Status:** **rolled out 2026-08-27 — 28/28 repaired + deployed to the `.env` instance, 0 flagged, PR #159.** The 2026-08-27 correction (see History): every copy now carries `alwaysInPdf="true"`+`summaryExclusion="true"`, +2/-0 per form. Run in 3 `--only` chunks (6 shape-diverse forms incl. AAOS_033, then 11 + 11 — run_sweep commits once per run, so a run dying late loses every form it had already deployed). Every form `delta ok` `diff ok` `verify ok` `deploy ok`. **Read back out of the live JCR** (bounded-depth node GETs, AAOS_033/BAWZ_033/AAPC_033/BBDY_033/AARB_033): the copy carries both attributes, `visible=false`, no `dorExclusion`, and is the **last child** of the last content step's `items`. Branch re-scan over the committed packages: **0 affected**, 0 manual. `check_regressions.py` over a fully repaired corpus copy: report **byte-identical** to the same run on the unrepaired base, so the two attributes trip no neighbour rule (6 rules are red on master today for unrelated reasons — repeating-panel relabel, signature-name-fill). Unit tests 20/20. **Still left at "rolled out": the DoR has never been rendered.** 192.168.64.3 was unreachable again for this rollout and the `.env` instance cannot render one, so requirement (2) is confirmed structurally only — exactly the gap that let the first rollout ship 28 inert copies. Do not write the completion word the CI guard greps for on this line until a DoR shows the disclaimer once at the bottom of the last page. **Note on the `.env` instance:** the owner's hand-built reference packages `AAOS_033` and `AAOV_033` were installed there after this rollout, so **AAOS_033 on that instance is the reference, not the swept package** (its copy sits inside `PN_InternalBankUseOnly`, named `PN_ItalyInfobox`). Re-install from the branch with `scripts/review_pr.py --sweep infobox-dor-copy --only AAOS_033` before reading AAOS_033 off it. Previous rollout of the wrong shape, for the record: 2026-08-26, 28/28 deployed, PR #155 (merged) — nodes in place, rendering nothing.

## PROBLEM-metadata-name — Metadata textdraw control: name should be `metadataTextDraw`, not `metadata`
**Symptom:** the hidden metadata text-draw control (`sling:resourceType="ajila-forms-customers/ajila-forms-ubs/components/controls/metadata"`, `guideNodeClass="guideTextDraw"`, `_value="Metadata"`, `visible="false"`, `dorExclusion="true"`) carries the AF element `name="metadata"` on some forms. The element **name** (the `name=` attribute, **not** the JCR node — the tag stays `<metadata>`) should be **`metadataTextDraw`**. Forms where the engine auto-generated a timestamped name (`name="metadata1718013683061"`) are **correct** and left untouched — only the exact value `metadata` is changed ("if it is, change it"). The corpus is two lineages, so a single form can carry **two** metadata controls (one legacy `name="metadata"`, one engine `name="metadata<ts>"`); only the first is touched. **222 of 286 packages affected** (227 occurrences); `name="metadata"` (exact) appears **only** on this control (0 false positives elsewhere), and nothing references it by name (the `var metadata = window.forms.ubs.getFormMetadata()` scripts + `metadataselector` attribute are unrelated), so the rename is a safe self-contained attribute edit. Changing only the attribute (not the node) keeps `fd:translationIds` `…##metadata##…` paths valid.
**Detect:** script: `find_attr_value.py --select controls/metadata --attr name --value metadata`  _(quotes removed 2026-07-27: with them, `detector_argv`'s `.split()` passed them through literally and this rule silently reported 0 affected — it was never actually enforced. Corpus verified clean either way.)_  _(affected = forms with a `controls/metadata` open tag whose `name` equals exactly `metadata`; exact match, so `metadata<ts>` is not flagged — idempotent: a fixed form reports 0)_
**Fix:** script: `set_attr_value.py --select 'controls/metadata' --attr name --from metadata --to metadataTextDraw`  _(quote-aware open-tag rewrite: on `controls/metadata` tags, change `name="metadata"` → `name="metadataTextDraw"`; timestamped siblings untouched; prints `{inner, matched, changed, remaining, ok}` — `ok` = no `name="metadata"` remains on a metadata control; idempotent)_
**Verify:** must-not: `name="metadata"`  _(+ the fix's `ok`; a plain must-not is exact — it does not match `name="metadata1718013683061"`)_
**Max changed lines:** 4   _(one attribute value changed per affected control; form_diff counts an in-place line edit as remove+add = 2. Five _033 forms carry **two** `name="metadata"` metadata controls → 4 diff lines; the bound covers them. A single-control form should be 2.)_
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** manual (owner directive — the metadata textdraw element name should be `metadataTextDraw`)
**Tracking:** #55
**Status:** swept 2026-07-20 (222/222 fixed + deployed, PR #56) — pilot of 6 (AAAQ/AABH/AABN/AABO_019, AAEA/AAGZ_033) reviewed OK, then `--continue` for the remaining 216; every form `verify:ok` + `deploy:ok`, `delta:skip` throughout (engine MCP not built — a pure attribute-value edit can't introduce schema violations). **5 forms flagged `diff` only, all verified benign:** AAOF/AAOM/AAQD/AAQG/BANW_033 each carry **two** `name="metadata"` metadata controls (distinct panels), so both were renamed → 4 diff lines (bound raised 1→2→4 accordingly). AAEA_033 (one legacy + one engine-timestamped control) had only the legacy `name="metadata"` renamed, the `name="metadata1742297849078"` sibling left intact — confirming exact-value matching. Only the `name=` attribute changed (node tag stays `<metadata>`, `##metadata##` translationIds intact). Marked swept 2026-07-20 after PR #56 merged and `check_regressions.py` confirmed the detector reports 0 affected on master.

## PROBLEM-formcode-page-title — Form-root page title must be the bare form code
**Symptom:** the form-root page title — `jcr:title` on the `cq:PageContent` node of the form's `aftemplatedpage` (`sling:resourceType=".../pages/aftemplatedpage"`) — must equal the **bare form code** (engine `root.xml:16`: `jcr:title="{{ form_code }}"`, **engine-confirmed authoritative for this property** by the owner). The code is always present in form data as `formrange_code` on the hidden metadata control (single .content.xml holds both the page node and the form body). **113 of 286 packages deviate:** 109 carry the machine default `AF_<code>`, 4 carry a descriptive human title (AAAV/AACE/AACS/AACW) — **all overwritten with the bare code** per the owner's "always the code" rule (decision A, 2026-07-20). The 173 already-bare-code forms are left as-is. No form carries an `AF_`-prefixed `jcr:title` on any non-page node, so the fix's page-node target and the `must-not` guard cannot collide with panel/step titles.
**Detect:** script: `find_pagetitle_code.py`  _(affected = the `cq:PageContent` node's `jcr:title` != the form's `formrange_code`; emits `detail[form]={code,title}`. Exact compare, so bare-code forms report 0 — idempotent)_
**Fix:** script: `set_pagetitle_code.py`  _(quote-aware open-tag rewrite: on the `cq:PageContent` open tag, set `jcr:title` to the per-form `formrange_code` (derived from the form, no `--from/--to`); every other `jcr:title` untouched; prints `{inner, code, before, changed, ok}` — `ok` = the page node now carries `jcr:title="<code>"`; idempotent)_
**Verify:** the fix's `ok` (page title == form code) + a re-scan showing 0 affected ; must-not: `jcr:title="AF_[A-Z0-9]{3,6}"`  _(safe: `AF_` titles occur only on the page node; a per-form `must` can't be expressed corpus-wide, so `ok` is the real check)_
**Max changed lines:** 2   _(one in-place value edit = remove+add = 2 diff lines; flag anything more)_
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** engine-diff (engine `root.xml:16` emits `jcr:title="{{ form_code }}"`)
**Tracking:** #59
**Status:** swept 2026-07-20 (113/113 fixed + deployed, PR #60) — pilot of 6 (AAAB/AAAE/AAAI/AAAL/AAAM/AAAQ_019) reviewed OK, then `--continue` for the remaining 107; every form `verify:ok`+`deploy:ok`, 0 flagged, `delta:skip` throughout (no engine MCP — a lone `jcr:title` value edit can't introduce schema violations). The 4 descriptive-title forms (AAAV/AACE/AACS/AACW_019) were overwritten with the bare code per decision A and deploy-verified on AEM (e.g. AACW "Cost information for investments…" → `AACW`). Marked swept 2026-07-20 after PR #60 merged and `check_regressions.py` confirmed the detector reports 0 affected on master.
