That predicate is duck-typed on `.attrs`/`.children` so it accepts a node from any of the pipeline's parsers. **Consequence to know: if this entry is ever retired, the four call sites in `find_/set_step_title_panel.py` and `find_/set_jump_to_field.py` must be removed too** (see PROBLEM-formcalc-to-js for how a retired entry leaves its scripts behind). Measured after the exemption: all 94 converted forms clean, and no pre-existing violation masked.
**THE FORM CONFIGURATOR'S HEADING IS NEVER A SUBTITLE (owner directive 2026-09-01).** It belongs to
the configurator. The four hand-reviewed reference forms — AAOS_033, AAOV_033, AATP_033, ABFH_033 —
all keep it as a `headingLevel="2"` titledraw (`css="stepTitle"` where present) inside the
`PN_FormConfigurator*` panel. `is_form_configurator()` excludes it from the conversion candidates
**and** from the "already converted" evidence, matched on the TEXT (`form config…`) and on the NAME
of the draw or of the panel directly enclosing it — deliberately not on every ancestor, because on
AARG_033 the whole first page sits inside `PN_FormConfigurator_a2439641` and an unbounded walk would
disqualify the real subtitle too. Measured over the 304 packages on the 97 pre-banking subtitle
candidates: **26 match the text, all 26 also match the name** (24 on the draw, 9 on the enclosing
panel), and **0** headings whose text is not "Form config…" match by name — the two signals agree
and neither over-reaches.

**26 forms already had it converted by an earlier run of this sweep and need a manual revert**
(reported as `review: form-configurator-converted`; the heading has to go back to a
`headingLevel="2"` titledraw inside the configurator panel, which no fixer here does): AAAA_019
AABT_033 AACT_019 AAEA_033 AAFO_019 AAFU_019 AAGA_019 AAGB_019 AAGY_019 AAHG_019 AAKG_019 AAKO_019
AALN_019 AALP_019 AALQ_019 AALR_019 AAOC_033 AAPT_033 AAQM_033 AAQQ_033 AARB_033 AASV_033 AATA_033
AATF_033 AATH_033 BAGT_019. Consequence to know: on those 26 the converted draw is what makes
`is_converted_step()` exempt the page, so PROBLEM-step-title-panel and PROBLEM-jump-to-field-button
are skipping those steps for a reason that is about to stop being true — **after the revert they come
back into both rules' scope.**

**Conversion candidates are `headingLevel="2"` ONLY, as of 2026-09-01.** `heads` used to take any
heading level. That was harmless only because a page whose subtitle was already converted returned
`already` before the heading list was consulted — and excluding the form configurator removed that
cover: AATH_033 immediately offered its `headingLevel="4"` draw "Modulo 3", a form-number label, as
the single remaining candidate. Every one of the 94 shapes this sweep was measured on is an h2. Side
effect: AAQR_033 (the Skip line) is no longer even a `flag` — its four "headings" are `h3` section
titles (`Sender`, `Receiver`, `Date and references`, `Letter content`), so the owner's "leave as it
is" is now the rule's own verdict. The Skip line is kept as the record of that decision.

**The bank's legal-entity line is not a subtitle either.** "UBS Europe SE (Succursale Italia)" is the
complementary text the owner puts **inside `PN_BR`**, under the banking-relationship fragment —
AAOS_033, AATP_033 and ABFH_033 all have it as a direct child of `PN_BR`. AAOV_033 is the one that
keeps it before `PN_BR`, as a sibling of the real subtitle. It is reported as
`review: succursale-text-outside-pn-br` on its own terms rather than as a missing subtitle class,
because telling someone to make that line a subtitle is wrong advice. 8 forms: AABI_019 AACJ_019
AACX_019 AACX_033 AAHA_019 AATH_033 AAWX_033 BBEY_033.

**THREE MORE SHAPES THIS RULE DOES NOT CONVERT ARE REPORTED, FLAG-ONLY (owner decision 2026-09-01).** The
conversion handles exactly one shape — a `headingLevel="2"` draw before the page-1 banking fragment.
Marco reported three it does not: a subtitle that never gets wrapped in a content panel, one that never
gets the CSS class, and a subtitle written as **two** text elements instead of one. Asked whether to
automate them, he chose **flag-only**: there is no reference shape for a merged subtitle, and a wrong
merge destroys a real heading (the lesson PROBLEM-fragment-title-duplicate paid for on ABHM_019).
`find_banking_subtitle.review()` emits, per form, independently of the form's `classify` verdict:

- `no-content-panel` — a subtitle draw (converted, or still an h2) that **no** panel encloses. The
  target shape KEEPS the enclosing `{step}Title` panel because that node carries
  `jumpToFieldButtonVisible`; where there is none the conversion leaves a bare static text with no
  panel and no button, and the fixer does not invent one. **6 in the corpus** (AAAM_019 ×2, AAGG_019,
  AAGY_019, AAPO_033, AARW_033).
- `no-css-class` — a class-less, level-less static text before the banking fragment that reads as a
  heading. The conversion never sees it (it looks for `headingLevel="2"`), so it gets neither the class
  nor a panel. **19 in the corpus.** The "reads as a heading" test is calibrated on the 94 subtitles
  already converted: **none** of them ends in `.`/`:`/`;`/`,`, median 16 characters, p90 39, 5 over 80 —
  so the test is ≤80 characters, no line break, no sentence-ending punctuation, and not a parenthetical
  (`(auszufüllen von jedem Kontoinhaber)` is a note, not a heading). 22 of the corpus's 57 class-less
  pre-banking draws pass. **It is a heuristic and it is allowed to be: every hit is a line in a report
  for a person, never an edit.**
- `two-text-elements` — two or more subtitle candidates before the banking fragment. **3 forms**
  (AAAM_019, AAFK_019, BAGJ_019); the other three were the form configurator or the Succursale line
  counted as a second subtitle.

**62 findings in 53 forms; `affected` stays 0 and no fixer reads any of it** —
`form-configurator-converted` 26 · `no-css-class` 19 · `succursale-text-outside-pn-br` 8 ·
`no-content-panel` 4 · `two-text-elements` 3. Calibration: on the four hand-reviewed reference forms
the detector reports **0 affected** and exactly one finding, AAOV_033's Succursale placement. `check_regressions.py`
grew an `advisory` channel for this: a detector may return a `review` list, which is collected, printed
as a `ℹ️` line and carried in the `--json` output under `advisory`, and which **never** affects `ok` —
a flag-only finding is not a regression, and making one turn the guard red would leave CI permanently
failing with no action available. Review surface: `find_banking_subtitle.py --form <F> --verbose`.

**Skip:** AAQR_033 (4 headings before page-1 banking, none marked `stepTitle` — owner: leave as it is, 2026-08-24)
**Runs automatically:** no
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-08-24 — DoR/PDF visibility)
**Tracking:** #139
**Status:** swept 2026-08-26 — **two passes, 94 forms.** **Panel-title pass 2026-08-26 (89/89 fixed + deployed to the `.env` instance, 0 flagged, PR #156):** the wrapper panel must not carry a `jcr:title` (owner directive). Piloted 7 chosen for variant coverage — AAOV_033 (the form the sweep came from), AACB_033 (wrapper named `PN_Titolare`, not `{step}Title`), AAFK_019 (extra `ubs-margin-20` token, panel tag on one line), AAAM_019 (multi-heading page-1), AABU_019 (page already carried a second subtitle-ish text), AACT_019 (panel title differed in case from the draw), BANR_033 (panel title differed from the draw text) — then the remaining 82 in **5 `--only` chunks of ≤20** (one commit + deploy per chunk: `run_sweep` commits once per run, so a long run dying late would lose every form it had already deployed). Every form `delta:ok diff:ok verify:ok deploy:ok`. **Verified independently of the runner's return codes:** a re-scan of the branch corpus reports **0 affected** (94 `already`, 1 `flag` = the AAQR_033 Skip); all **89** wrapper panels were **read back from the deployed JCR** — 89/89 with no `jcr:title`, the panel `name` unchanged, and `jumpToFieldButtonVisible="true"` still present wherever the package has it (AACT_019 has none by design — it is the form-configurator step, which PROBLEM-jump-to-field-button leaves buttonless); the full regression guard on the branch is **clean on every enrolled rule**. Diff per form is one removed attribute (1 changed line; 2 where the panel tag shares a line). — Previous pass, **rolled out 2026-08-25** (94/94 fixed + deployed, 0 flagged, PR #144 merged `de72960a`) — every form `delta:ok` `diff:ok` `verify:ok` `deploy:ok`; `check_regressions.py` confirmed 0 affected on master under the then-current rule (the panel title was not yet part of the target shape). AAQR_033 stays on the Skip line (4 headings, none marked — owner: leave as it is). **DoR/PDF confirmation sits with the customer (owner, 2026-08-25):** the premise of the sweep is that an h2 step title does not render in the finished DoR while a `subtitle-after-form-title` static text does. That cannot be checked from this environment — generating the finished DoR is only possible on the customer's side — so the rollout was accepted on the owner's directive for the target shape plus structural evidence (94/94 converted, `verify:ok` + `deploy:ok` throughout, no new guard violations, nothing masked, idempotent). **If the customer reports the subtitle still does not appear in the DoR, revert `de72960a` and re-run** — do not assume the shape is wrong until then, and do not treat this as an outstanding local task. **Open question carried into that conversation:** the converted draw keeps `dorExclusion="true"`/`summaryExclusion="true"` in 93 of 94 forms, so with the panel title now gone the heading may have no DoR representation at all; the owner's 2026-08-26 decision was the panel-title strip alone, and clearing the draw's exclusion is a separate call.
**Pilot review notes (2026-08-26 panel-title pass):** on screen the page must look **unchanged** — same heading, once, in the same place (the strip only removes a non-rendering panel attribute); and the **jump-to-field button must still be there** on that panel, since it is the panel whose title was removed. In the DoR, check whether the heading is still present — see the open question on the Status line. — **Pilot review notes (2026-08-25 conversion pass):** the DoR/PDF must actually show the subtitle. `AAFK_019` keeps `ubs-margin-20` and `AAPQ_033` keeps `widget_ajila-forms-ubs-textbox ubs-textbox-multiline` next to the new class (both `review_css`) — the latter styles a static text as an input, so check it. `AABU_019` and `BBQG_033` already carry a subtitle-ish static text before the banking fragment and will end up with two — check for visual duplication.

## PROBLEM-fragment-title-duplicate — A fragment's title repeated on the panel that references it
**Symptom:** a fragment can carry its own heading (a `jcr:title` on a panel **inside** the fragment), and AEM also lets you set a `jcr:title` on the panel that **references** the fragment. When both say the same thing the heading renders **twice**, on screen and in the DoR. The fix removes the **outside** copy (the referencing panel in the form); the fragment is shared by many forms and is never edited from this repo.
**Extent (128 of 304 packages, 129 instances):** `affrg_germany_InternalBankUse_OURef_Signature` 67 · `affrg_italy_internalbankuse_ouref` 57 · `affrg_germany_InternalBankUse` 2 · `affrg_italy_internalbankuse` 1 · `affrg_italy_internal_bank_use_cognome_luogo_data_visto` 1 · `affrg_global_InternalBankUse_Text_OURef_Signature` 1. Duplicated texts: 128× "For internal bank use only", 1× "Spazio riservato al soggetto collocatore («bene firma»)". One form (AAPQ_033) carries **two** such panels — hence 129 instances across 128 forms.
**Fragment titles are vendored data, not corpus-derivable.** Fragment packages live outside this repo, so the titles come from `.claude/scripts/data/fragment_panel_titles.json`, regenerated by hand with `data/extract_fragment_panel_titles.py <export.zip …>` whenever UBS ships new fragments. That tool deliberately (a) reads **both** export layouts — a real content package (`jcr_root/content/forms/af/…`) and a bare folder zip with no `jcr_root`, which is what AEM refuses with *"File is not a content package. Missing 'jcr_root'."* though it is still a readable content tree; (b) records a fragment with no title as **`[]` rather than dropping it**, so a consumer can tell "renders no title" (clean) from "not in the export" (unknown) — conflating those makes a stale map look like a clean corpus. The detector surfaces the latter as `unknown_fragment` (currently 0); **if that number rises, refresh the data file before trusting a run.** `fragment_titles.json` (PROBLEM-repeatable-add-label) could not be reused: it keeps only a fragment's *first* title and drops anything failing `is_sane` (>4 words or a stop word), which drops every title this rule needs.
**Detect:** script: `find_fragment_title_dupe.py`  _(emits `dupes[]` {form,panel,title,fragment,form_path,form_dir} — the review surface — plus `unknown_fragment[]` and counts. Matches case/whitespace-insensitively against **any** title the fragment renders. Field labels are excluded when the map is built (a `jcr:title` on a textbox is a label, and a panel repeating one is not this defect), as is the `cq:PageContent` node (its `jcr:title` is the fragment's asset name). `--form <F>`, `--verbose`. **Idempotent**: the fix removes the attribute, so a fixed panel has no title left to match.)_
**Fix:** script: `set_fragment_title_dupe.py` _(delegates the decision to the detector's `classify()`; byte-offset splice dropping the whole ` jcr:title="…"` attribute from the referencing panel's open tag, applied back-to-front. Refuses to touch a title whose panel has `jcr:title` in `fd:translationIds` — that would orphan a translation — reporting it under `flagged` instead; 0 such cases in the corpus today. Prints `{removed[],flagged[],well_formed,recheck_dupes,ok}`.)_
**Verify:** the fix's `ok` (every duplicate gone + result re-parses) + a re-scan reporting 0 affected + **count the title in the rendered form**, which is the one hard signal that separates a real duplicate from a field label: `curl -s -u <creds> '<preview URL>' | grep -o -i '<title>' | wc -l` must drop by exactly the number of panels fixed and must NOT reach 0. Measured on AAPQ_033 (two panels): 3 → 1. Plus a pilot visual check of the DoR/PDF.
**Max changed lines:** omit — one attribute removed per instance (1–2 lines); gate on `ok` + the re-scan.
**Measured before rollout:** fixer run on all 128 (on copies) → 128/128 `ok`, 129 titles removed, 0 failures, idempotent (second run byte-identical); **no new guard violations and nothing masked**, notably `repeatable-add-label` stays clean even though most of these panels are repeatables.
**A field label is not a title — the extractor whitelists `guideNodeClass`, never blacklists resourceType.** First cut excluded field labels by blacklisting resourceType words; `.../controls/signature` (a `guideScribble`) matches none of them, so **58 signature fragments** contributed **79** bogus "titles" (`Signature of client`, `Firma del cliente 1…6`, …). A false positive then needs a form whose referencing panel happens to carry that exact text — which happened **once**, on ABHM_019 (`PN_SignatureClientAdvisor`), where the sweep removed a REAL heading: the render went 5 → 3 and the 3 survivors were the field label, not a heading. Caught in pilot review by the owner. Adding `signature` to the blacklist would have fixed that one case and left the next unlisted control type to do the same, so the rule is now a whitelist: only `guideNodeClass="guidePanel"` carries a fragment title. Measured over both exports, nodes with a `jcr:title`: 715 guideTextBox · 132 guideDatePicker · 93 guideDropDownList · 90 guideScribble · **85 guidePanel** · 13 guideButton · 9 guideTelephone · 8 guideNumericBox · 4 guideRadioButton · 4 guideCheckBox. **Do not turn this back into a blacklist.**
**Origin:** manual (owner report 2026-08-25 — fragments acquired their own titles, so a manually-set title on the referencing panel now duplicates)
**Tracking:** #142
**Status:** swept 2026-08-25 (128/128 fixed + deployed, 129 titles removed, 0 flagged, PR #143 merged `caa77e9a`) — every form `delta:ok` `diff:ok` `verify:ok` `deploy:ok`; `check_regressions.py` confirms the detector reports 0 affected on master. AAPQ_033 carried two such panels, hence 129 instances across 128 forms.
**Pilot review notes:** the heading must show once in the form AND in the DoR/PDF. Watch the repeatables, where the removed panel title may have been doing layout work; `AAQK_033` is the only non-English case. Piloted 2026-08-25 on AAAI_019/AACB_033/AAAB_019/AAPQ_033/AAQK_033/BASA_019 (one per affected fragment) — ABHM_019 was in that pilot too and is what surfaced the whitelist bug; it is no longer affected and was restored in AEM.

## PROBLEM-static-text-dor-excluded-step — Static text directly in a `dorExcludeTitle` panel is missing from the DoR
**Symptom:** a panel whose title is excluded from the Document of Record (`dorExcludeTitle="true"` — on a wizard step this is the engine convention: the title lives in the `{step}Title` sub-panel; nested content panels carry it too) renders **none** of the static texts (`controls/textdraw` / `controls/titledraw`, `guideNodeClass="guideTextDraw"`) that are its **direct** children in the DoR. The same texts render when they sit in a content sub-panel (engine shape — AAOV `PN_Info_Last › PN_fa70f69d`) or when the step keeps its title placeholders (the QA's working AABT export: its steps with direct static texts all lack `dorExcludeTitle`). Found on **AAOV_033** `PN_Dichiarazione_2c49eddc` (the whole "Dichiarazione" declaration absent from the DoR); attribute-by-attribute comparison against the rendering `PN_Info_Last` step showed every attribute identical at every level — the only difference is the nesting. In the generated XDP the non-rendering step is `[title subform, draw, draw]`; the rendering ones are `[title subform, subform{draws}]` or `[AF_PANELSPACE, AF_PANELTITLE|placeholder, draw…]`. The mechanism inside XFA/the DoR pipeline is not established; the rule is the owner's. **Scope = every panel** under a wizard step, the step included (owner decision 2026-08-26, widened from the wizard-steps-only scope of 2026-08-25).
**Detect:** script: `find_static_text_wrap.py`  _(affected = a form with ≥1 **fix** run: a maximal run of adjacent static draws directly under a **target panel** — any panel with `dorExcludeTitle="true"` (and not itself `dorExclusion="true"`) reached by walking each wizard step's subtree depth-first (a `dorExclusion="true"` panel ends the walk: nothing below it reaches the DoR; FormMetadata/summary/preview steps are not walked) — of which at least one draw reaches the DoR (no `dorExclusion="true"`). Two draw kinds are run *separators*, never members: `headingLevel="2"` step titles (PROBLEM-step-title-panel's concern) and the banking-subtitle text (`css` carries `find_banking_subtitle.TARGET_CSS`). Reported separately, **not** as affected, so the rule can reach 0: `manual` — a draw is referenced by dotted path in a rule (`Parent.ST_x`; 1 form, AAIZ_019); `flag` — a draw carries a grid width other than 12 (a full-width wrapper would change the on-screen row; 16 runs in 7 forms). Idempotent: once wrapped the draws are no longer direct children (the wrapper carries no `dorExcludeTitle`, so it is never a target). Every row carries `step` (the wizard step), `parent` (the panel holding the run; == step at depth 0) and `depth`. Survey 2026-08-25 (steps only): 236 of 304 packages (151 × 019, 85 × 033), 767 runs / 1'718 draws; largest run AAAV_019 `PN_Vereinbarung` (94 draws) — swept in #148. **Survey 2026-08-26 (widened, on the post-#148 corpus): 113 of 304 packages (65 × 019, 48 × 033), 655 fix runs / 985 draws, all nested (depth 1–7); `flag` 340 runs in 41 forms (was 16 in 7), `manual` 97 runs in 47 forms (was 1)** — the multi-column rows and dotted-path rules that kept nested panels out of scope before are now *reported*, still not edited. Heavy forms: AACW_019 114 runs / 161 draws (single-text table-row panels `PN_Stp*` at depth 5–6, plus 94 skipped runs), AACE_019 83, AACC_019 53, BAGO_019 42, AAAM_019 26; 81 of the 113 forms have ≤3 runs.)_
**Fix:** script: `set_static_text_wrap.py`  _(shared logic `static_text_wrap.py`, unit tests `tests/test_static_text_wrap.py`. Each **fix** run is replaced by ONE engine-shaped content panel (`profiles/ubs/aem/panel.xml`, non-page: UBS custom panel, `dorExcludeDescription="true"`, `dorFieldStyling="Default"`, single-column columnar layout, width 12) named `PN_StaticText_<8 hex>` — deterministic from (target panel, first draw) so a re-run yields the same name, unique within the form (a shared panel name — the naming sweep's accepted exceptions — lengthens the hash instead of colliding). The draws are moved **byte-for-byte** (translation ids, rules, responsive widths untouched). `manual`/`flag` runs are left alone and listed under `skipped`, which makes `ok` false so the row shows ⚠ with the reason. Byte-offset splice via `set_step_title_panel`'s span helpers (quote-aware — `_value="&lt;p>…"` carries a literal `>`).)_
**Verify:** _no regex gate — the fix's own `ok` (result well-formed, no fix run left, nothing skipped) is the check, plus the pilot **DoR check**: the wrapped texts (AAOV "Dichiara/no e riconosce…" and "Iscritto all'Albo…") appear in the DoR._ **The DoR cannot be rendered on the local AEM** (`Cannot render untrusted template in protected mode`) and **a package install does not regenerate the shipped `dorTemplate` rendition** — so the visual gate runs on an instance where DoR generation works, after that instance has regenerated the template from the wrapped form (an editor save does; a bare install does not). If that pipeline renders the shipped rendition as-is, this fix is invisible there until the renditions are dropped or regenerated — 
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-08-25: "the issue only happens when the dorExcludeTitle is checked. In that case, wrap the static text within that panel in a new panel such that the static text is not a direct child of that panel. If multiple static texts are adjacent, put them all into the same new panel." Reference = the QA's working AABT export (Italy_AABT.zip, 2026-08-17) vs AAOV_033. **Widened 2026-08-26** on the owner's instruction to apply to all panels, not just root/wizard panels.)
**Tracking:** #147
**Ordering:** independent of the other sweeps: the wrapper is not a title panel (name does not end in `Title`, holds no h2 draw), so PROBLEM-step-title-panel and PROBLEM-jump-to-field-button ignore it; it is the UBS custom panel, so PROBLEM-panel-type-ubs is satisfied; `PN_` prefix satisfies the naming rule. Confirmed by `check_regressions.py` on the pilot branch.
**Status:** **rolled out 2026-08-26 (112/113 fixed + deployed to the `.env` instance, PR #154; 62 ✓, 50 ⚠ verify — every ⚠ is a skipped `flag:width` or `manual:dotted` run, everything else in those forms wrapped; `delta`/`diff`/`deploy` ok throughout)** — piloted 6 (AAOV_033 ✓, AACW_019 ⚠, AACE_019 ⚠, AAPC_033 ⚠, BAZA_019 ⚠, AACB_033 ⚠) on the owner's go-ahead, then AAOS_033 on request, then the rest in **6 `--only` chunks of ≤20** (one commit + deploy per chunk). **Deferred: AAIQ_019** — committed on `sweep/minor-occurs` (PR #150); run it with `--only AAIQ_019` after #150 merges (LFS zips cannot 3-way merge), then this entry can take the guard-enrolled wording (`check_regressions.py` enrols on that word; until AAIQ_019 is done the corpus is not at 0 affected). The five pilot ⚠ are by design: their fixer report lists skipped runs (AACW 94 / AACE 91 `flag:width` multi-column table rows, AAPC / BAZA / AACB 2 `manual:dotted` each in repeatable `RCP_*` panels whose `ST_Client*` texts are addressed by dotted path), everything else in them wrapped. Awaiting human review of the pilot (on-screen tables in AACW/AACE, DoR on the rendering instance) before `--continue` on the remaining 107. Widened 2026-08-26 (deliberately not the guard-enrolled wording: `check_regressions.py` enrols any Status line containing that word, and with the wider detector the guard would turn red on master for the 113 newly affected forms; restore the enrolled wording once the rollout PR merges). Code: `static_text_wrap.py` walks every panel under each step, detector/fixer emit `parent`/`depth`, 7 new unit tests (19 total, green). Fixer trialled on scratch copies of AACW_019 (114 wrapped, 94 skipped, ⚠), AAOV_033 (1 nested run, ok), AAPC_033 and BAZA_019 (3 wrapped, 2 skipped each): all well-formed, second run wraps nothing. **Pilot review must look at the on-screen tables**: AACW/AACE/AACC wrap a single static text per table-row panel, so a wrapper lands inside every row — confirm the rows still render as rows and the DoR shows the texts. Also unverified for nested panels: the `headingLevel="2"` separator rule assumes an h2 draw is a step title; inside a nested panel that premise may not hold (such a draw stays a direct child and stays out of the DoR). — Previous status, 2026-08-25, steps-only scope: rolled out 236/236 (fixed + deployed to the `.env` instance, 4 ⚠ verify — AAFN_033, AABI_019, AACH_019, AAEU_019: their width≠12 runs were skipped by design, everything else in those forms wrapped; PR #148) — piloted 6 (AAOV_033 reported form, AAPC_033, AABT_033, AAAV_019 94-draw run, AAAZ_019, AAFN_033 flag path), then rolled out in **12 `--only` chunks of 20** via a driver (one commit + deploy per chunk). Branch re-scan: 0 affected; remaining `flag` runs only in AAAA_019, AABI_019, AACH_019, AACS_019, AAEU_019, AAFN_033 (width) and `manual` in AAIZ_019 (dotted-path rule) — these are reported, not affected, so the CI guard is honest; they need a human decision (wrapper carrying the draw width / rewriting the rule path). Regression guard on the branch: all 25 enrolled rules clean. **The DoR-side outcome is unverified** — the `.env` instance cannot render a DoR and a package install does not regenerate the shipped `dorTemplate` rendition: confirm on the instance that renders DoRs (regenerate the template there first) that AAOV's "Dichiara/no e riconosce…" now appears; if it does not although the regenerated template nests it, the shipped rendition is what renders and the renditions have to be regenerated or dropped there.

## PROBLEM-static-text-orphan-step — A static text whose parent is the step itself is an orphan
**Symptom:** a static text (`controls/textdraw` / `controls/titledraw`, `guideNodeClass="guideTextDraw"`) sitting as a **direct child of a wizard step** has no panel of its own, and Redacto mis-renders it in the PDF. Every text has to live inside a UBS panel, so each orphan is wrapped in one and adjacent orphans share it. Corpus 2026-09-02: **49 orphan texts in 23 runs across 15 packages**, of the 107 texts that are direct children of a step in 40 packages. The rest: 34 share a row and 2 are addressed by dotted path (a human decides, below), 10 draws and 7 more inside 9 `dorExclusion` steps never reach the PDF, 4 are the banking subtitle and 2 the step's own heading. Scope = the direct children of every wizard step (FormMetadata/summary/preview steps are not walked); panels **below** the step are out of scope — their texts already have a panel, and reaching into them is the neighbouring rule's business, for its own reason.
**Two draws stay direct children of the step** and act as run separators: the step's own heading (`css="stepTitle"`, PROBLEM-step-title-panel and PROBLEM-step-title-single own it — 2 in the corpus) and the banking subtitle (`css` carries `find_banking_subtitle.TARGET_CSS`, which PROBLEM-banking-subtitle puts on the first page on purpose — 4). An `h2` that is **not** the step title is ordinary content and is wrapped with the rest (10 in the corpus, every one of them in a step that already has its title panel) — this is where the rule parts company with PROBLEM-static-text-dor-excluded-step, which reads every `h2` as a step title.
**Nothing that never reaches the PDF is touched:** a step carrying `dorExclusion="true"` (9 steps) and a run whose draws all carry it (10 runs) have nothing to mis-render.
**Detect:** script: `find_static_text_orphan.py`  _(affected = a form with ≥1 **fix** run: a maximal run of adjacent orphan texts directly under a wizard step, of which at least one reaches the PDF. Reported separately, **not** as affected, so the rule can reach 0: `manual` — a draw is referenced by dotted path in a rule (`Parent.ST_x`), which moving it would break (1 run, AAIZ_019); `flag` — a draw carries a grid width other than 12, so it shares its row with what sits beside it and a full-width wrapper would break the row (34 draws in 16 runs, 6 forms: AAAA/AABI/AACH/AACS/AAEU_019, AAFN_033 — checklist rows, two-column paragraph pairs, `Per il ___` lines). Idempotent: a wrapped draw's parent is the wrapper, not the step. 2026-09-02: **15 affected** (AAAM, AAAQ, AABU, AACX, AAGD, AAMC, ADEC, BAGY_019, AABT, AAGZ, AAQK, AAQT, AAWW, ABFH, BAVP_033); biggest runs ADEC_019 `PN_1781760329702` (16 definitions) and AACX_019 `PN_Definitions` (6).)_
**Fix:** script: `set_static_text_orphan.py`  _(shared logic `static_text_orphan.py`, unit tests `tests/test_static_text_orphan.py` (15). Each **fix** run becomes ONE engine-shaped content panel — `static_text_wrap`'s `wrapper_bytes`/`panel_name`/`wrap_runs`, i.e. the same `PN_StaticText_<8 hex>` UBS panel the neighbouring rule builds, deterministic from (step, first draw) and unique in the form. The draws are moved **byte-for-byte** (translation ids, rules, responsive widths untouched). `manual`/`flag` runs are left alone and listed under `skipped`, which makes `ok` false so the row shows ⚠ with the reason.)_
**Verify:** _no regex gate — the fix's own `ok` (result well-formed, no fix run left, nothing skipped) plus a re-scan showing 0 affected, and the pilot **PDF check**: the wrapped texts come out of Redacto in the right place, and the step still reads the same on screen._
**Max changed lines:** omit — a wrapper is a ~6-line insert around a run that is counted as remove+add; gate on `ok` + the re-scan.
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-09-02: "los textos que no estén dentro de un panel de UBS, es decir, su parent sea el propio panel que sea un step del formulario, son huérfanos … porque hay problemas de renderización en el PDF por Redacto")
**Tracking:** #168
**Ordering:** independent, and it composes with PROBLEM-static-text-dor-excluded-step rather than fighting it: the wrapper carries no `dorExcludeTitle`, so that rule never targets it, and a draw this rule wraps stops being a direct child of the step for it too. Neighbour rules on the two pilots: the wrapper is the UBS custom panel (PROBLEM-panel-type-ubs), its name takes the `PN_` prefix (PROBLEM-naming-conventions), it is not a title panel (PROBLEM-step-title-panel, PROBLEM-jump-to-field-button) and it carries neither `dorExclusion` nor `summaryExclusion` (PROBLEM-dor-exclusion-implies-summary). `blueprint:validate_aem_package_from_file` delta on AABT_033 (12 → 12) and ABFH_033 (16 → 16): **no new violation**, and the wrapper's `<items>` carries `gridFluidLayout2` so it adds no "items is missing sling:resourceType".
**Status:** swept

## PROBLEM-dor-exclusion-implies-summary — Everything excluded from the DoR must also be excluded from the summary
**Symptom:** a node carries `dorExclusion="true"` but no `summaryExclusion`, so content that is deliberately kept out of the Document of Record still appears in the summary. Owner directive (2026-08-26): **everything that is excluded from the DoR must also be excluded from the summary** — no exceptions by component type. The mechanism behind it: with `redactoSummary="true"` the UBS DoR is rendered by Redacto from the server-built *summary* HTML, where the exclusion switch is `summaryExclusion`; `dorExclusion` alone leaves e.g. the Italy infobox visible in the rendered DoR (AAOS_033 report). **The engine is not the reference** — it emits both attributes on its step-title node (`panel.xml:55-60`) and root nodes (`root.xml:201-202`) but `dorExclusion` only on field controls, so its own output is inconsistent; the directive is. Corpus 2026-08-26: **7'207 open tags in all 304 packages** lack the summary flag; 1'860 nodes already carry both (the target shape); the value is always the plain string `true` (never `{Boolean}true`), and 130 nodes are summary-only (not in scope). Per form min 8 (AADB_019) / median 17 / max 93 (AAIQ_019). Population by component: UBS panel 1'606 (643 `fragRef`), buttons 1'520 (remove/tertiary/submit/next/prev/guidebutton/preview), textbox 1'104 + textboxMultiline 167 + carousel 145, draws 1'270 (textdraw, metadata, dorOptionsUBS, titledraw, infobox, errorbox, messagebox), dropdownlist 203, radiobutton 115, datepicker 43, telephone 10, numericbox 1. **Review note:** the directive also covers *input* fields that were DoR-excluded but still showed on the on-screen summary step (`TXT_IBAN` AADI_019, `RB_ClientType`, letterhead `AF_*` fields) — after the fix they leave the summary step too; that is the intended reading, confirm it on the pilot.
**Detect:** script: `find_missing_attr_on.py --select 'dorExclusion="true"' --require-attr summaryExclusion=true`  _(reused, no new code; affected = any form with a `dorExclusion="true"` open tag that lacks `summaryExclusion="true"`; quote-aware, so rich-text `_value` with a literal `>` is safe. Single-quoted: all three parsers `shlex.split` the verb since 2026-08-26 — this entry is what exposed the divergence (unquoted, `run_sweep` saw `dorExclusion=true`, which only matches inside 39 forms' rule scripts, e.g. `ST_Letto.dorExclusion=true;`, and the real 304 went undetected).)_
**Fix:** script: `set_attr_on.py --select 'dorExclusion="true"' --set-attr summaryExclusion=true`  _(reused; inserts one `summaryExclusion="true"` line right after the `dorExclusion` line, same indent; single-line tags get it inline; existing values are left alone; prints `{inner, matched, added, updated, ok}`; idempotent — a fixed form reports `added:0, ok:true`.)_
**Verify:** the fix's own `ok` (every `dorExclusion="true"` open tag now carries `summaryExclusion="true"`) + a re-scan showing 0 affected + a pilot **visual check in AEM**: the summary step no longer lists DoR-excluded content (AAOS_033: infobox gone; a form with a DoR-excluded input field: that field gone from the summary) — and, on the instance that renders DoRs, the DoR no longer shows it. A regex gate is not used: a per-tag "has A but not B" condition is not expressible as a corpus-wide `must-not` pattern, and the detector re-scan is the honest check. Pre-checked on a fully fixed local corpus copy (2026-08-26): detector 304 -> 0, all 35 swept rules green in `check_regressions.py`.
**Max changed lines:** 100  _(purely additive, exactly one inserted line per affected node — `form_diff` `changed` equals the fixer's `added`; corpus max is 93 in AAIQ_019, so a form over 100 is a new shape worth a look, not a failure.)_
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-08-26; the engine is inconsistent and not the reference)
**Tracking:** #151
**Ordering:** touches **every** package, so its branch conflicts at merge (LFS, no 3-way) with every in-flight sweep branch — currently `sweep/minor-occurs` (#150). Merge the others first, then `--continue` on the merged base before merging this one. Neighbour rules: PROBLEM-jump-to-field-button strips `summaryExclusion` only from title panels that also lose `dorExclusion`, so a fixed corpus does not trip it (verified green); nothing else reads `summaryExclusion`.
**Status:** swept 2026-08-26 (304/304 fixed + deployed to the `.env` instance, 0 ⚠, PR #152) — piloted 6 (AAOS_033 the reported infobox-in-DoR form, AABT_033, ABFH_033, AAAA_019, AADB_019, AAHM_af_ab_019; JCR read back 27/14/33 nodes all carrying `summaryExclusion`), then rolled out in **15 `--only` chunks of 20** (one commit + deploy per chunk). Chunk 11 had 5 AEM installs fail with a packmgr traceback (AATV_033, AATW_033, AATZ_019, AAUB_033, AAUJ_033; the fixed bytes were already on the branch) — re-installed from the branch via `scripts/review_pr.py --sweep dor-exclusion-implies-summary --only <F>`, JCR read back 32/32 and 13/13. Branch re-scan: 0 affected / 304 already_ok; `check_regressions.py` on the branch with this rule enrolled: all rules clean. The pilot exposed the Detect-verb quoting divergence between `run_sweep` and `check_regressions` (fixed in the same PR). Merge `sweep/minor-occurs` (#150) first, then re-run `--continue` here on the merged base before merging.

## PROBLEM-summary-step-redacto — Every form must render its DoR through Redacto (summary step + `redactoSummary`)
**Symptom:** the UBS DoR is rendered by Redacto from the server-built **summary** HTML, and only when the DAM asset carries `redactoSummary="true"`. **60 of 304 packages** do not carry it. The flag alone is not the fix — with no summary component in the form, Redacto would build the DoR from nothing. Three parts have to hold together, so they are one rule: (1) `redactoSummary="true"` on the DAM asset's `<metadata>` node; (2) a `summaryPanel` step (`<summarypanel>`, UBS custom panel) holding the `controls/summary` component named `summaryComponent`, with the hidden `doroptionsubs` + `controls/metadata` controls inside it (engine R5.4); (3) the toolbar `nextitemnav` running `window.ajila.forms.ubs.control.summary.setSummaryData(guideRootPanel);` **before** `nextStep`, or the component is never populated. Corpus 2026-08-26: **239 complete · 5 have (1)+(2) but not (3)** (AAIJ/AAKM/AAMK/BAGT/BAXT_019 — their DoR renders from an unpopulated summary) **· 60 have none of the three**. The flag and the summary component correlate perfectly: no form carries one without the other. **Reference = the engine with `use_summary = "true"`** (`profiles/ubs/aem/root.xml:92-191`, `dam.xml:31`), owner-confirmed for this problem; the synthesised block is copied from the **deployed** reference AAAV_019, i.e. that shape *after* PROBLEM-dor-exclusion-implies-summary, which the raw template predates. Owner decisions (2026-08-26): build the full step, not just the flag; and **keep the carousel preview step** — the target is the shape 109 forms already have (summary step *and* preview step), not the engine's preview removal. The 5 lineage forms that name their preview step `PN_Preview` instead of `preview` are handled by name alias, not by tag.
**The heading follows the language rule every UI string in this repo follows.** `Summary of form information` is a dictionary key, so a form that ships a dictionary in its own language gets the English master and each locale translates it; the 52 packages with no dictionary get the wording in their own language (`Riepilogo delle informazioni del modulo`, `Zusammenfassung der Formulardaten`), because there the master is the text on screen and nothing can change it. The wordings come from `data/translations/`, the same table PROBLEM-default-translations keeps the dictionaries in step with.
**The summary step must match the reference form exactly** (AAOS_033, 2026-09-01), bar the per-form data: the panel's own attributes (`visible="{Boolean}true"` and `dorFieldStyling="Default"` were missing from 300 and 291 of them), and its five children in order — `messagebox_ElsigCheck`, `summaryComponent`, `submitErrorMessage`, `doroptionsubs`, `metadataTextDraw`. The last two are the converter's, under generated names (`doroptionsubs1742297849012`, `metadata1761809308844`), and are renamed rather than re-created: the metadata carries the form's own `formrange_code` and its `entities/item0/cdoks`, which are never taken from the reference. A second copy of either inside the panel is dropped, unless it is a metadata whose form code differs from its twin's — that is for a person.
**The toolbar's preview button goes with it.** Redacto renders the preview its own way; 159 toolbars still carried one, spelled `preview` or `previewbutton…` under a generated name.
**Detect:** script: `find_summary_step.py`  _(affected = any form missing any of redacto / panel / summaryComponent / hook; derived from the **post-fix** shape, so an unswept form and a half-fixed one both flag. Emits `detail[form]={bucket,missing,anchor,doroptionsubs,metadata}` — buckets `missing-panel` (60) and `missing-hook` (5). Idempotent: a complete form reports 0.)_
**Fix:** script: `set_summary_step.py`  _(inserts the `<summarypanel>` block directly before the preview step, **moving** the form's existing `doroptionsubs` + metadata controls into it, prepends the `setSummaryData` SCRIPTMODEL to the `nextitemnav` `fd:click` array ahead of whatever is already there, and inserts `redactoSummary="true"` into the DAM metadata node in FileVault's alphabetical attribute order. Each part applied only when missing → idempotent. Transform is the pure function `apply(form_bytes, dam_bytes)`; 15 unit tests in `tests/test_summary_step.py`. Prints `{inner, dam, actions, moved, ok, flag?}` — `ok` is re-derived from the OUTPUT with the detector's own `state_from_bytes`, and a flagged form is written back **unchanged**.)_
**Verify:** the fix's own `ok` (the output satisfies all four detector conditions) + a re-scan showing 0 affected + a pilot **visual check in AEM**: the new Summary step lists the entered values, the preview/carousel step still works, and — on the instance that renders DoRs — the DoR comes out through Redacto rather than empty. A corpus-wide regex gate is not used: "has a summary panel containing a summary component" is not expressible as a `must-not` pattern, and the fixer's `ok` plus the detector re-scan are the honest check.
**Max changed lines:** 220   _(the panel is a ~110-line insert plus the ~50 moved lines counted as remove+add, plus 1 hook line and 1 DAM line; measured across the 60: min 137, median 157, max 201 (BANS_019). A hook-only form is exactly 2. Anything over 220 is a new shape worth a look.)_
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-08-26: "enable redacto for all forms"; engine confirmed as the reference for this property)
**Tracking:** #157
**Ordering:** no overlap with the only in-flight sweep branch, `sweep/minor-occurs` (#150, 7 forms) — checked 2026-08-26. Neighbour rules verified green on a fully fixed local corpus copy (all 34 swept detectors clean): the new panel carries `summaryExclusion="true"` next to `dorExclusion="true"` for PROBLEM-dor-exclusion-implies-summary; `summaryPanel` is already in PROBLEM-naming-conventions' `EXEMPT_PANEL_NAMES`; PROBLEM-step-title-panel / -jump-to-field-button / -banking-* all skip a panel tagged `summarypanel`, so the new step needs no step-title wrapper; PROBLEM-toolbar-nav-handler holds `nextitemnav` out of scope, and this fix only prepends to its `fd:click` (the 25 forms with an extra carousel-hide script and the 1 on the new `window.forms.ubs` namespace keep what they have). The moved `doroptionsubs` keeps its `fd:translationIds` verbatim: that id is an opaque dictionary key (it names `previewPanel`, which matches no node in the tree today) and rewriting it would orphan the dictionary entry.
**Status:** swept 2026-08-26 (65/65 fixed + deployed to the `.env` instance, 0 ⚠, PR #158) — piloted 6 chosen to cover every shape (AAAA_019 `preview` anchor + **no** `messagebox_ElsigCheck` today, AAAI_019 with one, AACB_033 `PN_Preview` anchor / other lineage / 31 steps, BAWZ_033 _033 without an ElsigCheck, AAIJ_019 the `missing-hook` bucket at a 2-line diff, BANS_019 the largest diff at 201 lines), reviewed, then rolled out in **3 `--only` chunks of 20/20/19** (one commit + deploy per chunk). Every form `delta:ok diff:ok verify:ok deploy:ok` — `delta` ran for real throughout (engine MCP present), so the synthesised panel introduces no AEM-schema violation. Pilot JCR read-back on all 6: `redactoSummary=true`, `summaryPanel` directly before the preview step, children in order `messagebox_ElsigCheck · summaryComponent · submitErrorMessage · doroptionsubs · metadata`, `setSummaryData` on `nextitemnav`, and the moved controls present exactly once. Branch re-scan: **0 affected / 304 complete**; `check_regressions.py` on the branch with this rule enrolled: all rules clean.

## PROBLEM-preview-step-removed — The carousel preview step is gone
**Symptom:** the preview used to be a step of its own holding a `carousel`, a few messageboxes and, on some forms, the `doroptionsubs` and metadata controls. Redacto renders the preview its own way, so the step is obsolete — **145 of 304 packages** still carry one, named `preview` (139) or `PN_Preview` (6).
**Three things go together,** which is why they are one rule: the step with everything in it; the statements elsewhere that name the carousel (26 forms run `initializeForPreview(carouselPreview, …)` from the toolbar's `nextitemnav`, and a statement naming a node that no longer exists throws — which in this runtime costs every statement after it in the same rule); and nothing lost with it — where the step still holds the only copy of the form's `doroptionsubs` or metadata control, the rule refuses and says so.
**Detect:** script: `find_preview_step.py`  _(per form `ok`, `remove` or a flag naming what the step still holds; affected = any step or any statement left)_
**Fix:** script: `set_preview_step.py` _(byte-offset splices: cut the step, then rewrite each rule value that names the carousel — statements removed line by line, a document left with nothing but comments dropped from the value list, an event left with no document loses the attribute)_
**Verify:** the fix's own `ok` (no step and no statement left, output parses) + a re-scan showing 0 affected + a pilot **visual check in AEM**: the wizard goes from the last content step straight to the summary, Next still works from every step, and the DoR still comes out through Redacto.
**Max changed lines:** omit — the step is a ~100-line delete; gate on `ok` + the re-scan.
**Ordering:** runs **after** PROBLEM-summary-step-redacto, which moves the `doroptionsubs`/metadata controls out of the step and into the summary panel, and deletes the toolbar's preview button. Run alone, 57 forms flag because the step still holds those controls.
**Runs automatically:** no
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-09-01: the preview step and its button are obsolete under Redacto)
**Tracking:** #TBD
**Status:** swept

## PROBLEM-internal-bank-use-pdf-only — Internal-bank-use / spazio-riservato fragment panels reach the reader only through the PDF
**Symptom:** the panels referencing the "for internal bank use only" / "spazio riservato al soggetto collocatore" fragments (8 fragRefs: `germany/affrg_germany_InternalBankUse[_OURef_Signature]`, `italy/affrg_italy_internalbankuse[_ouref]`, `italy/affrg_italy_InternalBankUse_Text_OURef_Signature`, `italy/affrg_italy_internal_bank_use_cognome_luogo_data_visto`, `global/affrg_global_InternalBankUse_Text_OURef_Signature`, `global/affrg_global_spazio_riservato_al_soggetto_collocatore`) are meant for the bank's copy only: never on screen, never on the summary step, always in the PDF. In the corpus they are ordinary visible panels, and 12 Italy forms toggle them from a "FIM signature verification" checkbox with `window.forms.ubs.showAFShowDor(P)` / `hideAFHideDor(P)` (AABT/AAEA/AAKP/AAOF/AAOI/AAOM/AAQM/AARB/AAUK also carry a separate Initialize value `P.visible = true;`; AAQK toggles `P.dorExclusion`), which shows the panel on screen and never sets the property the summary builder reads. **Reference = AAOS_033 as re-authored by the owner on 2026-08-27** (not the engine): per panel `summaryExclusion="true"` + `alwaysInPdf="true"` + `visible="{Boolean}false"`, and every statement that toggled such a panel becomes `P.jsonModel.alwaysInPdf = "true" | "false"`. Mechanism (ajila-forms-ubs `summary.js pushData`): a `summaryExclusion` node is skipped unless `alwaysInPdf` is set, and is then built hidden in the HTML, so only the Redacto PDF carries it. **fragRefs are deliberately not changed** (owner decision 2026-08-27; the market-to-global routing of this family is not part of PROBLEM-fragment-library-consolidation either, whose table lists all six market fragments as `kind: other`). Extent 2026-08-27: **166 panels in 153 of 304 packages** (82 x 019, 71 x 033), **148 forms affected** (AATV_033, AATW_033, BAGZ_033, BAVP_033 already complete; AACH_019 only `manual`), **55 rule statements in 12 forms** (5 distinct bodies), 1 panel reported `manual` (AACH_019 `FormMetadata`, a mis-named copy carrying `dorExclusion="true"`: the author excluded it from the DoR outright, `alwaysInPdf` would reverse that, so a human decides).
**Detect:** script: `find_internal_bank_use_pdf.py`  _(affected = a family panel lacks one of the three attributes, or an `fd:scripts` value anywhere in the form still toggles a family panel through a visibility verb (`showAFShowDor`/`hideAFHideDor`/`showAFHideDor`/`hideAFShowDor`, `.visible =`, `.dorExclusion =`). Reported separately, NOT as affected: `manual` — a `dorExclusion="true"` family panel, a visual-editor `fd:rules` value naming such a panel, or a statement shape the fixer does not rewrite (indexed instance path, a rule that READS the panel's visibility). `--form <F>`, `--verbose`. Idempotent: rewritten statements carry no verb, the original sits in a `/* was: … */` comment that is blanked before matching.)_
**Fix:** script: `set_internal_bank_use_pdf.py`  _(shared logic `internal_bank_use_pdf.py`, unit tests `tests/test_internal_bank_use_pdf.py`. Quote-aware open-tag splice: the three attributes are inserted in FileVault's alphabetical position (a present one at another value is updated); every `fd:<event>` value on `fd:scripts` nodes is decoded with `visible_init.decode`, the statement rewritten in the SCRIPTMODEL body (`showAFShowDor(P)`/`hideAFShowDor(P)` -> `"true"`, `hideAFHideDor(P)`/`showAFHideDor(P)` -> `"false"`, `P.visible = v` -> `v`, `P.dorExclusion = v` -> `!v`; only when the target path's last segment is a family panel of this form; comments and string literals never match) and re-encoded with `visible_init.encode`, so the JCR multi-value stays intact. The original statement is kept as a trailing `/* was: … */` block comment (the reference keeps it as a `//` line; a block comment keeps a brace-less `if` and same-line code intact). Statements about any other component are byte-identical. Prints `{inner, panels, names, attrs_added, attrs_updated, rules_rewritten, statements_rewritten, manual, well_formed, remaining, ok}`; `ok` = well-formed + own re-scan finds nothing + nothing manual. `--dry-run`.)_
**Verify:** the fix's own `ok` + a re-scan showing 0 affected + the pilot **visual check in AEM**: the panel is gone from the screen and from the summary step; on the instance that renders DoRs the internal-bank-use block IS in the PDF, and in the 12 FIM forms it follows the checkbox (ticked: present, unticked: absent). No regex gate: the panel names vary per form, and the detector re-scan is the honest check.
**Max changed lines:** 16  _(measured on the simulated corpus: 127 forms change 5 lines (one panel, three inserts), two-panel forms with rules 11, AAOS_033 14 — anything above is a new shape worth a look.)_
**Measured before the pilot (scratch copy of all 304 packages, 2026-08-27):** 149/149 fixer `ok` (run before AACH_019's `dorExclusion` panel became `manual`; it is untouched now), 462 attributes added, 3 updated (`visible="{Boolean}true"` -> false), 23 rule values / 55 statements rewritten, 0 manual, every package still `unzip -t` clean, re-scan 0 affected, second pass byte-identical, `validate_delta` clean on AAOS_033, `check_regressions.py` on the fixed copy: every enrolled rule green (PROBLEM-metadata-languages read red only because the scratch copy has no source PDFs; on the real corpus it is 0). Neighbour rules by construction: `configurator_reset` reads `showAFShowDor`/`.visible =` but only on radio/dropdown choices, the FIM control is a checkbox; `visible-on-init` reads `fd:visible` only; `dor-exclusion-implies-summary` is one-directional.
**Reference deltas deliberately NOT part of the rule:** the re-authored AAOS also dropped `dorExcludeTitle="true"` from `PN_FMsinatureinternal` and `minOccur="1" maxOccur="4"` from `PN_FRG_InternalBankUseOnly` (87 corpus panels are such 1..4 repeatables); treated as incidental pending the owner's word.
**Runs automatically:** yes
**Corpus fixed:** no — 142 of 148 fixed + deployed to the `.env` instance (PR #162); the 6 forms on the Skip line are committed on `sweep/minor-occurs` (#150) and get `--only` after that PR merges; AACH_019 stays `manual`.
**Origin:** manual (owner directive 2026-08-27; reference AAOS_033.zip re-authored by the owner)
**Tracking:** #161
**Ordering:** `origin/integration/repeating-panels-and-general-fragments` changes no package of its own (its zip diff against master was master's own commits), so no conflict there. `sweep/minor-occurs` (#150) edits 7 packages, 6 of them affected here; they are on the Skip line below and get `--only` once #150 merges. Every family panel is the UBS Content Panel already, no rename happens, so the naming and panel-type guards are unaffected.
**Skip (6 forms, 2026-08-27):** committed on `sweep/minor-occurs` (#150); run `--only` on them after that PR merges. THE FORM LIST MUST STAY ON THIS LINE. AAAN_019 AAAZ_019 AABA_019 AABF_019 AAIQ_019 BARS_019
**Status:** swept 2026-08-27 (142/148 fixed + deployed to the `.env` instance, 0 ⚠, PR #162; the 6 Skip-line forms follow after #150) — piloted 7 (AAOS_033, AAUK_033, AAQK_033, AAAL_019, AABE_019, BASA_019, BBPZ_019; JCR read back: 13/13 panels in target state, AAOS/AAUK/AAQK rules assign `jsonModel.alwaysInPdf`, every remaining verb mention inside a `/* was: … */` comment), then the remaining 135 in **7 `--only` chunks of 20** (one commit + deploy per chunk), every chunk `delta:ok` `diff:ok` `verify:ok` `deploy:ok`. Branch re-scan: affected = exactly the 6 Skip-line forms, 147 already_ok, 1 manual; `check_regressions.py` on the branch: every enrolled rule green. Deployed sample read back from the JCR (2 forms per chunk): 14/14 panels in target state. Remaining: `--only AAAN_019 AAAZ_019 AABA_019 AABF_019 AAIQ_019 BARS_019` after #150 merges, then drop the Skip line and set `Corpus fixed: yes`. The DoR-side outcome (block present in the PDF, following the FIM checkbox) is verified by the owner on the DoR-rendering instance, not here.
**Pilot review notes:** one form per shape: AAOS_033 (the reference form itself, two panels + FIM rules), AAUK_033 (FIM rules incl. the separate `P.visible = true` Initialize value, two panels), AAQK_033 (`dorExclusion` toggles, panel was `visible=true`), AAAL_019 (germany OURef panel inside the `PN_Signature` step), AABE_019 (1..4 repeatable OURef panel), BASA_019 (global fragment, only `visible` missing), BBPZ_019 (global fragment, `visible` already false). What to look at: the internal-bank-use block must not render on screen or on the summary step; on the DoR-rendering instance the PDF must carry it, and in AAOS/AAUK/AAQK it must appear only when the FIM checkbox is ticked. The repeatable case (AABE_019, and 86 more 1..4 panels) is the one to watch: a hidden repeatable's Add button is gone with it, so the PDF shows the declared minimum.
